Result: 实验表明，将基于结果的策略优化与知识蒸馏结合在单一目标中，相比离线训练、稀疏奖励GRPO等方法，在长文本推理上更稳定有效，且保持了短文本能力。

Conclusion: dGRPO为长文本对齐提供了改进的方案，结合奖励优化与密集教师指导能克服各自方法的局限性。

Abstract: Adapting large language models (LLMs) to long-context tasks requires post-training methods that remain accurate and coherent over thousands of tokens. Existing approaches are limited in several ways: 1) off-policy methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and knowledge distillation (KD) suffer from exposure bias and limited recovery from model-generated errors over long horizons; 2) on-policy reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) better align training with model-generated states, but are unstable and sample-inefficient due to sparse rewards; 3) on-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level guidance, but does not directly optimize arbitrary reward signals. In this paper, we propose Distilled Group Relative Policy Optimization (dGRPO), a method for long-context reasoning that augments GRPO with dense guidance from a stronger teacher via OPD. We also introduce LongBlocks, a synthetic long-context dataset spanning multi-hop reasoning, contextual grounding, and long-form generation. We conduct extensive experiments and ablations comparing off-policy training, sparse-reward GRPO, and our combined approach, leading to an improved recipe for long-context alignment. Overall, our results show that combining outcome-based policy optimization with knowledge distillation in a single objective provides a more stable and effective path to long-context reasoning, while preserving short-context capabilities.

</details>


### [181] [Mind the Pause: Disfluency-Aware Objective Tuning for Multilingual Speech Correction with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12242)
*Deepak Kumar,Baban Gain,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种多语言语音识别文本不流利修正流水线，结合序列标注、指令微调与对比学习，在印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语上取得优于强基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR转录文本中的不流利现象（填充词、重复、假起始）降低可读性，影响下游系统可靠性；现有检测-移除策略破坏语法结构和语义连贯性，而LLM相关研究多聚焦于检测或数据增强，未实现全面修正。

Method: 提出多语言修正流水线：1) 序列标注器标记不流利词元；2) 利用这些信号指导LLM进行指令微调以重写为流畅文本；3) 引入对比学习目标，惩罚复制不流利词元的行为，保留语法和语义。

Result: 在印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语三种印度语言上，该方法持续优于包括多语言序列到序列模型在内的强基线。

Conclusion: 仅检测策略不足以解决不流利修正问题；结合词元级线索、指令微调与对比学习为多语言不流利修正提供了实用且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) transcripts often contain disfluencies, such as fillers, repetitions, and false starts, which reduce readability and hinder downstream applications like chatbots and voice assistants. If left unaddressed, such disfluencies can significantly degrade the reliability of downstream systems. Most existing approaches rely on classical models that focus on identifying disfluent tokens for removal. While this strategy is effective to some extent, it often disrupts grammatical structure and semantic coherence, leading to incomplete or unnatural sentences. Recent literature explored the use of large language models (LLMs); however, these efforts have primarily focused on disfluency detection or data augmentation, rather than performing comprehensive correction. We propose a multilingual correction pipeline where a sequence tagger first marks disfluent tokens, and these signals guide instruction fine-tuning of an LLM to rewrite transcripts into fluent text. To further improve reliability, we add a contrastive learning objective that penalizes the reproduction of disfluent tokens, encouraging the model to preserve grammar and meaning while removing disfluent artifacts. Our experiments across three Indian languages, namely Hindi, Bengali, and Marathi show consistent improvements over strong baselines, including multilingual sequence-to-sequence models. These results highlight that detection-only strategies are insufficient. Combining token-level cues with instruction tuning and contrastive learning provides a practical and scalable solution for multilingual disfluency correction in speech-driven NLP systems. We make the codes publicly available at https://github.com/deepak-kumar-98/Mind-the-Pause.

</details>


### [182] [PreScam: A Benchmark for Predicting Scam Progression from Early Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12243)
*Weixiang Sun,Shang Ma,Yiyang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zehong Wang,Colby Nelson,Xusheng Xiao,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PreScam是一个基于用户提交诈骗报告构建的对话诈骗进展建模基准，包含11,573个实例和20个诈骗类别，层次化标注了诈骗生命周期、心理操作和受害者回应。实验表明，监督编码器在实时终止预测上显著优于零样本LLM，而下一动作预测对所有模型都具挑战性，揭示了当前模型在理解诈骗逐步升级和操纵展开方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注静态诈骗检测或合成诈骗，缺乏对真实世界对话诈骗随时间推移进展的理解能力。作者旨在探究语言模型能否理解多轮对话中诈骗的渐进式发展过程。

Method: 从用户提交的177,989条原始诈骗报告中筛选构建了11,573个对话诈骗实例，涵盖20个诈骗类别。每个实例依据提出的诈骗杀伤链（scam kill chain）进行层次化结构标注，并在轮次级别标注诈骗者的心理操作和受害者回应。设计了两个评测任务：实时终止预测（判断对话是否接近终止阶段）和诈骗者动作预测（预测诈骗者的后续行为）。

Result: 监督编码器在实时终止预测任务上显著优于零样本LLM；下一动作预测即使对强LLM也仅取得中等成功。这表明当前模型能捕捉部分诈骗相关线索，但在追踪风险升级和跨轮次操纵展开方面仍存在困难。

Conclusion: 表面级别的流畅性与进展建模之间存在明显差距，现有模型对诈骗的渐进式理解和预测能力有限，需要进一步研究以提升对多轮对话诈骗的动态建模能力。

Abstract: Conversational scams, such as romance and investment scams, are emerging as a major form of online fraud. Unlike one-shot scam lures such as fake lottery or unpaid toll messages, they unfold through multi-turn conversations in which scammers gradually manipulate victims using evolving psychological techniques. However, existing research mainly focuses on static scam detection or synthetic scams, leaving open whether language models can understand how real-world scams progress over time. We introduce PreScam, a benchmark for modeling scam progression from early conversations. Built from user-submitted scam reports, PreScam filters and structures 177,989 raw reports into 11,573 conversational scam instances spanning 20 scam categories. Each instance is hierarchically structured according to the scam lifecycle defined by the proposed scam kill chain, and further annotated at the turn level with scammer psychological actions and victim responses. We benchmark models on two tasks: real-time termination prediction, which estimates whether a conversation is approaching the termination stage, and scammer action prediction, which forecasts the scammer's subsequent actions. Results show a clear gap between surface-level fluency and progression modeling: supervised encoders substantially outperform zero-shot LLMs on real-time termination prediction, while next-action prediction remains only moderately successful even for strong LLMs. Taken together, these results show that current models can capture some scam-related cues, yet still struggle to track how risk escalates and how manipulation unfolds across turns.

</details>


### [183] [What makes a word hard to learn? Modeling L1 influence on English vocabulary difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12281)
*Jonas Mayer Martins,Zhuojing Huang,Aaricia Herygers,Lisa Beinborn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究使用梯度提升模型，基于词频、意义、表面形式和跨语言迁移等特征，预测母语为西班牙语、德语或中文的英语学习者的词汇难度。通过Shapley值分析发现，词频（熟悉度）是所有语言学习者共同的主导因素，但西班牙语和德语学习者还依赖正字法迁移，而中文学习者则仅依赖熟悉度和表面特征。模型可提供可解释的、针对不同母语的难度估计，用于词汇课程设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究词汇难度的决定因素，以及这些因素如何因学习者的母语（L1）不同而变化，从而为不同语言背景的英语学习者提供个性化的词汇学习支持。

Method: 使用梯度提升模型（gradient-boosted models）对母语为西班牙语、德语或中文的英语学习者的词汇难度进行建模，特征包括词频（熟悉度）、意义、表面形式和跨语言迁移。利用Shapley值计算各组特征的重要性。

Result: 词熟悉度（如频率）是所有三种语言学习者共同的主导特征。西班牙语和德语学习者的难度预测还依赖于正字法迁移（orthographic transfer），而中文学习者无法利用这一迁移机制，其难度仅由熟悉度和表面特征共同决定。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了可解释的、针对不同母语（L1）定制的词汇难度估计，可用于设计个性化的词汇教学课程。

Abstract: What makes a word difficult to learn, and how does the difficulty depend on the learner's native language? We computationally model vocabulary difficulty for English learners whose first language is Spanish, German, or Chinese with gradient-boosted models trained on features related to a word's familiarity (e.g., frequency), meaning, surface form, and cross-linguistic transfer. Using Shapley values, we determine the importance of each feature group. Word familiarity is the dominant feature group shared by all three languages. However, predictions for Spanish- and German-speaking learners rely additionally on orthographic transfer. This transfer mechanism is unavailable to Chinese learners, whose difficulty is shaped by a combination of familiarity and surface features alone. Our models provide interpretable, L1-tailored difficulty estimates that can be used to design vocabulary curricula.

</details>


### [184] [TokenRatio: Principled Token-Level Preference Optimization via Ratio Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12288)
*Truong Nguyen,Tien-Phat Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Duy Minh Ho Nguyen,Khoa Doan,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Token级Bregman偏好优化（TBPO），通过token级Bradley-Terry模型和Bregman散度密度比匹配目标，仅用序列级成对比较数据恢复token级偏好最优性，在多项基准上优于序列级和token级基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO基于序列级偏好建模，但文本生成本质上是逐token决策过程，现有token级方法通常仅将序列级Bradley-Terry目标分解到时间步，未显式建模每个前缀（状态）下的最优性，作者希望仅使用标准序列级成对比较数据恢复token级偏好最优性。

Method: 提出TBPO框架，建立基于前缀条件化下一个动作的token级Bradley-Terry偏好模型，推导Bregman散度密度比匹配目标（推广逻辑回归/DPO损失），保持token级模型诱导的最优策略和DPO的简洁性。给出两个实例：TBPO-Q显式学习轻量级状态基线，TBPO-A通过优势归一化移除基线。

Result: 在指令遵循、有用性/无害性、摘要生成等基准上，TBPO相比强序列级和token级基线方法，提高了对齐质量和训练稳定性，并增加了输出多样性。

Conclusion: TBPO通过token级偏好建模和Bregman散度优化，在仅使用序列级偏好数据的情况下，有效恢复了token级最优性，实现了更好的对齐效果和训练稳定性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a widely used RL-free method for aligning language models from pairwise preferences, but it models preferences over full sequences even though generation is driven by per-token decisions. Existing token-level extensions typically decompose a sequence-level Bradley-Terry objective across timesteps, leaving per-prefix (state-wise) optimality implicit. We study how to recover token-level preference optimality using only standard sequence-level pairwise comparisons. We introduce Token-level Bregman Preference Optimization (TBPO), which posits a token-level Bradley-Terry preference model over next-token actions conditioned on the prefix, and derive a Bregman-divergence density-ratio matching objective that generalizes the logistic/DPO loss while preserving the optimal policy induced by the token-level model and maintaining DPO-like simplicity. We introduce two instantiations: TBPO-Q, which explicitly learns a lightweight state baseline, and TBPO-A, which removes the baseline through advantage normalization. Across instruction following, helpfulness/harmlessness, and summarization benchmarks, TBPO improves alignment quality and training stability and increases output diversity relative to strong sequence-level and token-level baselines.

</details>


### [185] [GKnow: Measuring the Entanglement of Gender Bias and Factual Gender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12299)
*Leonor Veloso,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GKnow基准，用于区分语言模型中的事实性别知识和性别偏见。研究发现性别偏见与事实性别在电路和神经元层面严重纠缠，导致消融作为去偏方法不可靠，且现有基准可能隐藏去偏过程中事实性别知识的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对性别神经机制的解释存在两个问题：(i) 过于聚焦特定性别任务（如代词预测），(ii) 未能区分基于语义属性的事实性别输出与基于刻板印象的性别偏见输出。

Method: 构建GKnow基准来评估语言模型在不同类型性别相关预测中的性别知识与性别偏见，识别和分析负责性别预测的电路和单个神经元，在DiFair、GKnow测试集和StereoSet上测试神经元消融的影响。

Result: 性别偏见和事实性别在电路和神经元层面严重纠缠，消融作为去偏方法不可靠；评估性别偏见的基准可能隐藏神经元消融伴随的事实性别知识下降。

Conclusion: 需要更稳健的性别偏见基准来区分事实性别与性别偏见，消融方法需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Recent works have analyzed the impact of individual components of neural networks on gendered predictions, often with a focus on mitigating gender bias. However, mechanistic interpretations of gender tend to (i) focus on a very specific gender-related task, such as gendered pronoun prediction, or (ii) fail to distinguish between the production of factually gendered outputs (the correct assumption of gender given a word that carries gender as a semantic property) and gender biased outputs (based on a stereotype). To address these issues, we curate \gknow, a benchmark to assess gender knowledge and gender bias in language models across different types of gender-related predictions. \gknow allows us to identify and analyze circuits and individual neurons responsible for gendered predictions. We test the impact of neuron ablation on benchmarks for disentangling stereotypical and factual gender (DiFair and the test set of GKnow), as well as StereoSet. Results show that gender bias and factual gender are severely entangled on the level of both circuits and neurons, entailing that ablation is an unreliable debiasing method. Furthermore, we show that benchmarks for evaluating gender bias can hide the decrease in factual gender knowledge that accompanies neuron ablation. We curate GKnow as a contribution to the continuous development of robust gender bias benchmarks.

</details>


### [186] [Overview of the MedHopQA track at BioCreative IX: track description, participation and evaluation of systems for multi-hop medical question answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12313)
*Rezarta Islamaj,Joey Chan,Robert Leaman,Jongmyung Jung,Hyeongsoon Hwang,Quoc-An Nguyen,Hoang-Quynh Le,Harikrishnan Gurushankar Saisudha,Ganesh Chandrasekar,Rustam R. Taktashov,Nadezhda Yu. Bizyukova,Sofia I. R. Conceição,Paulo R. C. Lopes,Reem Abdel Salam,Mary Adewunmi,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BioCreative IX MedHopQA基准测试发布了一个包含1000个双跳生物医学问答对的数据集，重点关注罕见疾病。48个提交系统通过RAG等检索增强策略显著优于基线（最佳系统MedCPT F1达89.30%，零样本基线为67.40%），概念级评估（MedCPT）相比表面字符串匹配能更准确评估答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学领域多跳问答仍面临重大挑战，需要系统整合多个来源的信息来回答复杂问题。现有基准缺乏针对多跳推理的高质量数据集，特别在罕见疾病方面存在空白。

Method: 构建了1000个两跳问答对，涵盖疾病、基因和化学品，每个问题需整合两个不同Wikipedia页面的信息。采用表面字符串匹配（EM）和概念准确性（MedCPT得分）两种评估指标。共48个提交来自13个团队。

Result: 最佳系统MedCPT F1达89.30%、EM达87.30%，显著优于零样本基线（MedCPT F1 67.40%、EM 60.20%）。RAG及检索增强策略是实现高性能的关键。概念级评估能更好处理表面形式不同但语义正确的回答。

Conclusion: RAG等检索增强策略对生物医学多跳QA至关重要；概念级评估优于纯字符串匹配；数据集已公开以推动该领域持续发展。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering (QA) remains a significant challenge in the biomedical domain, requiring systems to integrate information across multiple sources to answer complex questions. To address this problem, the BioCreative IX MedHopQA shared task was designed to benchmark in multi-hop reasoning for large language models (LLMs). We developed a novel dataset of 1,000 challenging QA pairs spanning diseases, genes, and chemicals, with particular emphasis on rare diseases. Each question was constructed to require two-hop reasoning through the integration of information from two distinct Wikipedia pages. The challenge attracted 48 submissions from 13 teams. Systems were evaluated using both surface string comparison and conceptual accuracy (MedCPT score). The results showed a substantial performance gap between baseline LLMs and enhanced systems. The top-ranked submission achieved an 89.30% F1 score on the MedCPT metric and an 87.30% exact match (EM) score, compared with 67.40% and 60.20%, respectively, for the zero-shot baseline. A central finding of the challenge was that retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and related retrieval-based strategies were critical for strong performance. In addition, concept-level evaluation improved answer assessment when correct responses differed in surface form. The MedHopQA dataset is publicly available to support continued progress in this important area. Challenge materials: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/research/bionlp/medhopqa and benchmark https://www.codabench.org/competitions/7609/

</details>


### [187] [Output Composability of QLoRA PEFT Modules for Plug-and-Play Attribute-Controlled Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12345)
*Michela Lorandi,Anya Belz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索了三种超越单任务训练/推理的PEFT泛化方法：多数据集联合训练、推理时组合PEFT模块权重、推理时组合PEFT模块输出。实验发现，组合PEFT模块输出是一种特别强的组合方法，在情感控制任务上三模块输出组合相比单任务专用模块平均提升2%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEFT技术虽能以较低成本进行特定任务微调，但每个新任务（组合）都需要单独微调，缺乏泛化能力。本文旨在探索如何让PEFT超越单任务训练/推理的局限，实现更高效的跨任务泛化。

Method: 在三种不同LLM上，以QLoRA为PEFT技术，针对情感控制、主题控制和多属性控制三组受控文本生成数据集，测试了三种泛化方法：(i)多相关数据集联合训练；(ii)推理时组合独立训练PEFT模块的权重矩阵；(iii)推理时组合独立训练PEFT模块的输出。

Result: 组合PEFT模块输出是最强的组合方法，在单任务测试集上甚至优于单任务专用模块，三模块输出组合在情感控制任务上平均提升2%的性能。

Conclusion: 推理时组合PEFT模块输出是一种一致性强且有效的泛化策略，能够在无需重新训练的情况下实现跨任务泛化，且性能优于或持平其他替代方法。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) techniques offer task-specific fine-tuning at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, but require separate fine-tuning for every new task (combination). In this paper, we explore three ways of generalising beyond single-task training/inference: (i) training on combinations of multiple, related datasets; (ii) at inference, composing the weight matrices of separately trained PEFT modules; and (iii) at inference, composing the outputs of separately trained PEFT modules. We test these approaches on three different LLMs, QLoRA as the PEFT technique, and three sets of controlled text generation datasets for sentiment control, topic control, and multi-attribute control. We find that summing PEFT module outputs is a particularly strong composition method, which consistently either outperforms or matches the performance of alternative approaches. This is the case even when comparing against single-task specialised modules on the single-task test set, where three-module output composition achieves an average 2% point performance increase across all models for sentiment control.

</details>


### [188] [MedHopQA: A Disease-Centered Multi-Hop Reasoning Benchmark and Evaluation Framework for LLM-Based Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12361)
*Rezarta Islamaj,Robert Leaman,Joey Chan,Nicholas Wan,Qiao Jin,Natalie Xie,John Wilbur,Shubo Tian,Lana Yeganova,Po-Ting Lai,Chih-Hsuan Wei,Yifan Yang,Yao Ge,Qingqing Zhu,Zhizheng Wang,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedHopQA是一个以疾病为中心的多跳推理基准，包含1,000个专家策划的问答对，要求模型整合跨两篇不同维基百科文章的信息，以开放式自由文本格式作答，并设计了抗污染、抗饱和的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学问答基准存在局限性：多项选择格式允许模型通过排除法而非推理作答；广泛使用的考试风格数据集面临性能饱和与训练数据污染风险；而多跳推理能力在临床诊断支持、文献基础发现和假设生成等任务中至关重要，却在当前基准中代表性不足。

Method: 构建包含1,000个专家策划的问答对，每个问题需整合两篇不同维基百科文章的信息；采用开放式自由文本格式；使用MONDO、NCBI Gene和NCBI Taxonomy本体锚定的同义词集支持词汇和概念级评估；通过人工标注、分诊、迭代验证和LLM-as-a-judge验证的结构化流程构建；将1,000个评分问题嵌入到10,000个问题的可下载数据集中，答案被隐藏，以减少排行榜博弈和污染风险。

Result: MedHopQA作为BioCreative IX的共享任务发布，提供了1,000个专家策划的多跳推理问答对，以及一个抗饱和、抗污染的评估框架。

Conclusion: MedHopQA既是一个基准测试，也是一个可复用的框架，将组合推理、抗饱和性和抗污染性作为核心设计约束，用于构建未来的生物医学问答数据集。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in the biomedical domain requires benchmarks that can distinguish reasoning from pattern matching and remain discriminative as model capabilities improve. Existing biomedical question answering (QA) benchmarks are limited in this respect. Multiple-choice formats can allow models to succeed through answer elimination rather than inference, while widely circulated exam-style datasets are increasingly vulnerable to performance saturation and training data contamination. Multi-hop reasoning, defined as the ability to integrate information across multiple sources to derive an answer, is central to clinically meaningful tasks such as diagnostic support, literature-based discovery, and hypothesis generation, yet remains underrepresented in current biomedical QA benchmarks. MedHopQA is a disease-centered multi-hop reasoning benchmark consisting of 1,000 expert-curated question-answer pairs introduced as a shared task at BioCreative IX. Each question requires synthesis of information across two distinct Wikipedia articles, and answers are provided in an open-ended free-text format. Gold annotations are augmented with ontology-grounded synonym sets from MONDO, NCBI Gene, and NCBI Taxonomy to support both lexical and concept-level evaluation. MedHopQA was constructed through a structured process combining human annotation, triage, iterative verification, and LLM-as-a-judge validation. To reduce leaderboard gaming and contamination risk, the 1,000 scored questions are embedded within a publicly downloadable set of 10,000 questions, with answers withheld, on a CodaBench leaderboard. MedHopQA provides both a benchmark and a reusable framework for constructing future biomedical QA datasets that prioritize compositional reasoning, saturation resistance, and contamination resistance as core design constraints.

</details>


### [189] [Context Convergence Improves Answering Inferential Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12370)
*Jamshid Mozafari,Bhawna Piryani,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在开放域问答中处理推理类问题的能力，提出使用**收敛性**（句子排除错误答案的有效程度）作为构建段落的标准。实验表明，基于高收敛性句子构建的段落能显著提升模型准确率，且按收敛性降序排列句子可进一步改善性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放域问答中广泛应用，但其处理推理类问题（需推导而非直接检索答案）的能力尚未被充分探索。现有段落构建方法多依赖余弦相似度等表面相关性，缺乏对推理过程有效性的考量。

Method: 使用TriviaHG数据集子集，以收敛性为准则组合句子构建段落。比较不同收敛性水平、余弦相似度基线以及句子排序方式对六个不同规模和架构的大语言模型在推理问答任务上的影响。

Result: 高收敛性句子构建的段落相比余弦相似度基线显著提升答案准确率；按收敛性降序排列句子能略微改善性能，表明大语言模型倾向于优先利用前面信息丰富的线索。

Conclusion: 收敛性是指导段落构建和分析大语言模型推理性推理行为的实用信号。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used in open-domain Question Answering (QA), their ability to handle inferential questions-where answers must be derived rather than directly retrieved-remains still underexplored. This study investigates how the structure and quality of passages influence LLM performance on such questions. We focus on convergence, a measure of how effectively sentences (hints) eliminate incorrect answers, as a criterion for constructing passages. Using subsets of the TriviaHG dataset, we form passages by combining sentences with varying convergence levels and evaluate six LLMs of different sizes and architectures. Our results show that passages built from higher convergence sentences lead to substantially better answer accuracy than those selected by cosine similarity, indicating that convergence captures meaningful relevance for inferential reasoning. Additionally, ordering sentences by descending convergence slightly improves performance, suggesting that LLMs tend to prioritize earlier, information-rich cues. These findings highlight convergence as a practical signal for guiding passage construction and analyzing inferential reasoning behavior in LLMs.

</details>


### [190] [Pretraining Exposure Explains Popularity Judgments in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12382)
*Jamshid Mozafari,Bhawna Piryani,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次基于完全可观测的预训练数据（OLMo模型及Dolma语料库，7.4万亿词元），直接大规模分析了LLM中的流行度偏差。研究发现：预训练曝光度与Wikipedia流行度强相关；LLM的流行度判断更接近于预训练曝光度而非Wikipedia，尤其在成对比较和大模型中更为显著；流行度先验主要由预训练统计量而非外部流行度信号塑造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对知名实体表现出系统性偏好（流行度偏差），但此前无法区分这种偏好是反映真实世界流行度还是预训练中的统计曝光度，因为大多数训练语料库不可访问。

Method: 使用开源OLMo模型及其完整预训练语料Dolma（7.4万亿词元），计算2,000个实体（涵盖Person, Location, Organization, Art, Product五类）的精确曝光度统计；与Wikipedia页面浏览量及两种LLM流行度信号（直接标量估计和成对比较）进行对比分析。

Result: 预训练曝光度与Wikipedia流行度强相关；LLM的流行度判断与预训练曝光度的对齐程度高于与Wikipedia的对齐，尤其在成对比较模式下；该对齐性在更大模型中更强，并在Wikipedia流行度不可靠的长尾分布中依然保持。

Conclusion: LLM中的流行度先验主要由预训练统计数据而非外部流行度信号塑造，证实数据曝光在驱动流行度偏差中起核心作用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic preferences for well-known entities, a phenomenon often attributed to popularity bias. However, the extent to which these preferences reflect real-world popularity versus statistical exposure during pretraining remains unclear, largely due to the inaccessibility of most training corpora. We provide the first direct, large-scale analysis of popularity bias grounded in fully observable pretraining data. Leveraging the open OLMo models and their complete pretraining corpus, Dolma, we compute precise entity-level exposure statistics across 7.4 trillion tokens. We analyze 2,000 entities spanning five types (Person, Location, Organization, Art, Product) and compare pretraining exposure against Wikipedia pageviews and two elicited LLM popularity signals: direct scalar estimation and pairwise comparison. Our results show that pretraining exposure strongly correlates with Wikipedia popularity, validating exposure as a meaningful proxy for real-world salience during the training period. More importantly, we find that LLM popularity judgments align more closely with exposure than with Wikipedia, especially when elicited via pairwise comparisons. This alignment is strongest for larger models and persists in the long tail, where Wikipedia popularity becomes unreliable. Overall, our findings demonstrate that popularity priors in LLMs are primarily shaped by pretraining statistics rather than external popularity signals, offering concrete evidence that data exposure plays a central role in driving popularity bias.

</details>


### [191] [Scalable Token-Level Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12384)
*Rui Min,Tianyu Pang,Chao Du,Minhao Cheng,Yi R. Fung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokenHD提出了一种用于训练token级幻觉检测器的全流程方法，包含可扩展的数据引擎和重要性加权的训练策略。即便仅0.6B的小模型，检测性能也超越了QwQ-32B等大型推理模型，且检测性能随模型规模从0.6B到8B一致提升，并展现出良好的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM幻觉检测方法在推理密集型任务中面临两个主要问题：(1) 步骤级分析粒度不足，(2) 依赖步骤分割导致可扩展性差。因此需要更细粒度的token级检测方案。

Method: TokenHD包含三个核心组件：(1) 可扩展的数据引擎，用于合成大规模token级幻觉标注；(2) 重要性加权训练策略，用于鲁棒模型训练；(3) 严格的评估协议。检测器可直接在自由文本上运行，无需预定义步骤分割或文本重格式化。

Result: 0.6B的小检测器经TokenHD训练后性能显著提升，超越QwQ-32B等大型推理模型；检测性能从0.6B到8B模型规模呈一致扩展；在多种实际场景中具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: TokenHD证明了token级幻觉检测的有效性和可扩展性，为无需步骤分割的细粒度幻觉检测提供了新范式，且可通过进一步策略增强跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities, but they still frequently produce hallucinations. These hallucinations are difficult to detect in reasoning-intensive tasks, where the content appears coherent but contains errors like logical flaws and unreliable intermediate results. While step-level analysis is commonly used to detect internal hallucinations, it suffers from limited granularity and poor scalability due to its reliance on step segmentation. To address these limitations, we propose TokenHD, a holistic pipeline for training token-level hallucination detectors. Specifically, TokenHD consists of a scalable data engine for synthesizing large-scale hallucination annotations along with a training recipe featuring an importance-weighted strategy for robust model training. To systematically assess the detection performance, we also provide a rigorous evaluation protocol. Through training within TokenHD, our detector operates directly on free-form text to identify hallucinations, eliminating the need for predefined step segmentation or additional text reformatting. Our experiments show that even a small detector (0.6B) achieves substantial performance gains after training, surpassing much larger reasoning models (e.g., QwQ-32B), and detection performance scales consistently with model size from 0.6B to 8B. Finally, we show that our detector can generalize well across diverse practical scenarios and explore strategies to further enhance its cross-domain generalization capability.

</details>


### [192] [A Comparative Study of Controlled Text Generation Systems Using Level-Playing-Field Evaluation Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12395)
*Michela Lorandi,Anya Belz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种公平比较不同受控文本生成（CTG）系统的标准化评估方法（LPF），通过对代表性系统重新评估后发现，大多数系统的性能远低于原报告结果，揭示了当前CTG领域缺乏标准化评估实践的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTG系统采用不同的数据集和评估方法，难以公平比较各系统的实际性能，因此需要一种既能提供信息又对各系统公平的评估方法。

Method: 采用水平竞技场（LPF）方法进行对比评估：(i) 以标准化方式生成和处理所有系统输出；(ii) 应用一套共享的评估方法和数据集（基于当前使用的方法选取），以确保公平评估。

Result: 对一组代表性CTG系统重新评估后，性能结果与原始报告结果存在显著差异，大多数情况下性能更差。

Conclusion: LPF评估揭示的差异表明CTG领域迫切需要标准化、可重复的评估实践；缺乏这种实践，已发表的性能声明可能会严重误导系统真实能力。

Abstract: Background: Many different approaches to controlled text generation (CTG) have been proposed over recent years, but it is difficult to get a clear picture of which approach performs best, because different datasets and evaluation methods are used in each case to assess the control achieved.
  Objectives: Our aim in the work reported in this paper is to develop an approach to evaluation that enables us to comparatively evaluate different CTG systems in a manner that is both informative and fair to the individual systems.
  Methods: We use a level-playing-field (LPF) approach to comparative evaluation where we (i) generate and process all system outputs in a standardised way, and (ii) apply a shared set of evaluation methods and datasets, selected based on those currently in use, in order to ensure fair evaluation.
  Results: When re-evaluated in this way, performance results for a representative set of current CTG systems differ substantially from originally reported results, in most cases for the worse. This highlights the importance of a shared standardised way of assessing controlled generation.
  Conclusions: The discrepancies revealed by LPF evaluation demonstrate the urgent need for standardised, reproducible evaluation practices in CTG. Our results suggest that without such practices, published performance claims may substantially misrepresent true system capabilities.

</details>


### [193] [Question Difficulty Estimation for Large Language Models via Answer Plausibility Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12398)
*Jamshid Mozafari,Bhawna Piryani,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-DAPS方法，通过计算候选答案 plausibility 分数的熵来估计问题难度，在多个QA数据集上优于基线方法，且具有鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问题难度估计方法（如可读性公式、检索信号、流行度统计）难以捕捉LLM在QA任务中面临的推理挑战，需要一种更有效的方法。

Method: Q-DAPS通过计算候选答案上 plausibility 分数的熵来度量问题难度，熵值越高表示问题越难。

Result: 在TriviaQA、NQ、MuSiQue、QASC四个数据集上一致优于基线；对超参数变化和问题类型具有强鲁棒性；消融实验表明在不同plausibility估计范式、模型规模和现实设置下均保持鲁棒；人类评估证实与人类难度判断高度一致。

Conclusion: Q-DAPS提供了一种可解释、可扩展且对偏差具有弹性的问题难度估计方法，适用于现代QA系统。

Abstract: Estimating question difficulty is a critical component in evaluating and improving large language models (LLMs) for question answering (QA). Existing approaches often rely on readability formulas, retrieval-based signals, or popularity statistics, which may not fully capture the reasoning challenges posed to modern LLMs. In this paper, we introduce Q-DAPS (Question Difficulty based on Answer Plausibility Scores) method, a novel approach that estimates question difficulty by computing the entropy of plausibility scores over candidate answers. We systematically evaluate Q-DAPS across four prominent QA datasets-TriviaQA, NQ, MuSiQue, and QASC-demonstrating that it consistently outperforms baselines. Moreover, Q-DAPS shows strong robustness across hyperparameter variations and question types. Extensive ablation studies further show that Q-DAPS remains robust across different plausibility estimation paradigms, model sizes, and realistic settings. Human evaluations further confirm strong alignment between Q-DAPS's difficulty estimates and human judgments of question difficulty. Overall, Q-DAPS provides an interpretable, scalable, and bias-resilient approach to question difficulty estimation in modern QA systems.

</details>


### [194] [Stories in Space: In-Context Learning Trajectories in Conceptual Belief Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12412)
*Eric Bigelow,Raphaël Sarfati,Daniel Wurgaft,Owen Lewis,Thomas McGrath,Jack Merullo,Atticus Geiger,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM的上下文学习可被视为低维几何空间（概念信念空间）中的信念轨迹，通过故事理解任务验证了信念更新的流形结构、表征解码及因果干预的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有研究将LLM的上下文学习视为贝叶斯推断，但对其背后的潜假设空间结构尚不清楚，本文旨在揭示这一结构。

Method: 以故事理解为动态信念更新的自然场景，结合行为分析与表征分析，研究LLM在低维概念信念空间中的信念轨迹。

Result: (1) 信念更新可描述为低维结构化流形上的轨迹；(2) 该结构在行为与内部表征中一致反映，可被线性探针解码；(3) 对表征的干预可因果性地引导信念轨迹，其效果可由概念空间的几何结构预测。

Conclusion: 为LLM中的信念动态提供了几何解释，将上下文学习的贝叶斯解释建立在结构化概念表征之上。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) update their behavior in context, which can be viewed as a form of Bayesian inference. However, the structure of the latent hypothesis space over which this inference operates remains unclear. In this work, we propose that LLMs assign beliefs over a low-dimensional geometric space - a conceptual belief space - and that in-context learning corresponds to a trajectory through this space as beliefs are updated over time. Using story understanding as a natural setting for dynamic belief updating, we combine behavioral and representational analyses to study these trajectories. We find that (1) belief updates are well-described as trajectories on low-dimensional, structured manifolds; (2) this structure is reflected consistently in both model behavior and internal representations and can be decoded with simple linear probes to predict behavior; and (3) interventions on these representations causally steer belief trajectories, with effects that can be predicted from the geometry of the conceptual space. Together, our results provide a geometric account of belief dynamics in LLMs, grounding Bayesian interpretations of in-context learning in structured conceptual representations.

</details>


### [195] [ORBIT: Preserving Foundational Language Capabilities in GenRetrieval via Origin-Regulated Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12419)
*Neha Verma,Nikhil Mehta,Shao-Chuan Wang,Naijing Zhang,Alicia Tsai,Li Wei,Lukasz Heldt,Lichan Hong,Ed Chi,Xinyang Yi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型在生成式检索微调中出现的灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出ORBIT方法，通过追踪微调前后模型权重的距离，并在距离超过阈值时采用权重平均策略来约束模型偏移，从而保留模型的通用推理与检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在针对特定任务（如生成式检索）进行微调时，会迅速丧失其通用的语言推理能力，即灾难性遗忘。现有方法对这一问题在GenRetrieval场景下的严重性认识不足。

Method: 提出ORBIT方法：在微调过程中主动追踪当前模型与初始模型权重的距离，当该距离超过预设最大阈值时，采用权重平均策略来约束模型参数漂移，从而抑制灾难性遗忘。

Result: ORBIT在保留文本生成能力和检索性能方面均优于常见的持续学习基线方法和同样使用权重平均的相关正则化方法。

Conclusion: 微调过程中的模型参数漂移与灾难性遗忘之间存在强相关性，ORBIT通过动态权重距离约束有效缓解了生成式检索微调中的通用能力遗忘问题。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancements in large language model (LLM) development, fine-tuning them for specific tasks often results in the catastrophic forgetting of their general, language-based reasoning abilities. This work investigates and addresses this challenge in the context of the Generative Retrieval (GenRetrieval) task. During GenRetrieval fine-tuning, we find this forgetting occurs rapidly and correlates with the distance between the fine-tuned and original model parameters. Given these observations, we propose ORBIT, a novel approach that actively tracks the distance between fine-tuned and initial model weights, and uses a weight averaging strategy to constrain model drift during GenRetrieval fine-tuning when this inter-model distance exceeds a maximum threshold. Our results show that ORBIT retains substantial text and retrieval performance by outperforming both common continual learning baselines and related regularization methods that also employ weight averaging.

</details>


### [196] [Predicting Disagreement with Human Raters in LLM-as-a-Judge Difficulty Assessment without Using Generation-Time Probability Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12422)
*Yo Ehara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种不依赖生成概率信号、基于嵌入空间几何一致性预测LLM难度评分与人类评分不一致的方法，在CEFR句子难度评估任务上优于概率基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自动生成教育材料日益普遍，但难度分配仍需大量人工；LLM-as-a-Judge虽受关注但与人评分者的一致性是主要挑战，现有概率信号方法难以跨模型比较。

Method: 利用难度标签的有序性，不依赖生成时概率信号，而是使用ModernBERT等独立嵌入空间，基于评分集的几何一致性（如排序一致性）识别可能与人评分不一致的LLM生成难度评级，将这类案例送交重新评分。

Result: 在基于CEFR的英语句子难度评估任务上，使用GPT-OSS-120B和Qwen3-235B-A22B两种LLM进行实验，所提方法在预测与人类评分者不一致方面取得了比概率基线方法更高的AUC。

Conclusion: 基于几何一致性的方法可以有效预测LLM难度评分与人类评分的分歧，避免了概率信号的跨模型不可比问题，为减少人工复核成本提供了新思路。

Abstract: Automatic generation of educational materials using large language models (LLMs) is becoming increasingly common, but assigning difficulty levels to such materials still requires substantial human effort. LLM-as-a-Judge has therefore attracted attention, yet disagreement with human raters remains a major challenge. We propose a method for predicting which LLM-generated difficulty ratings are likely to disagree with human raters, so that such cases can be sent for re-rating. Unlike prior approaches, our method does not rely on generation-time probability signals, which must be collected during rating generation and are often difficult to compare across LLMs. Instead, exploiting the fact that difficulty is an ordinal scale, we use a separate embedding space, such as ModernBERT, and identify disagreement candidates based on the geometric consistency of the rating set. Experiments on English CEFR-based sentence difficulty assessment with GPT-OSS-120B and Qwen3-235B-A22B showed that the proposed method achieved higher AUC for predicting disagreement with human raters than probability-based baselines.

</details>


### [197] [Geometric Factual Recall in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12426)
*Shauli Ravfogel,Gilad Yehudai,Joan Bruna,Alberto Bietti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了一种几何形式的记忆机制：在单层Transformer中，主题嵌入以线性叠加方式编码关联属性，MLP充当关系条件选择器（而非联想键值映射），仅需对数级别的嵌入维度即可记忆随机双射；并扩展至多跳推理场景，展示了容量-深度权衡与信息论下界，实验验证了梯度下降自动发现该结构且MLP可零样本迁移至全新双射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统观点——将Transformer内部权重矩阵视为联想记忆（参数数量与事实数量线性相关），探索更高效的几何记忆形式及其机制。

Method: 理论构造与证明（单层Transformer在受控设定下记忆随机双射，证明对数维度即可；多跳设定下的有无思维链的构造），信息论下界推导，以及梯度下降实验验证与零样本迁移测试。

Result: 发现嵌入维度只需对数级别即可完成记忆；MLP学到的是通用的关系条件选择机制而非具体事实；多跳场景下存在容量-深度权衡，并给出了匹配的信息论下界。

Conclusion: Transformer的记忆机制本质上是几何化的：嵌入编码关系结构，MLP作为选择器而非联想存储器，这一发现挑战了传统的联想记忆观点，并揭示了模型泛化能力的来源。

Abstract: How do transformer language models memorize factual associations? A common view casts internal weight matrices as associative memories over pairs of embeddings, requiring parameter counts that scale linearly with the number of facts. We develop a theoretical and empirical account of an alternative, \emph{geometric} form of memorization in which learned embeddings encode relational structure directly, and the MLP plays a qualitatively different role. In a controlled setting where a single-layer transformer must memorize random bijections from subjects to a shared attribute set, we prove that a logarithmic embedding dimension suffices: subject embeddings encode \emph{linear superpositions} of their associated attribute vectors, and a small MLP acts as a relation-conditioned selector that extracts the relevant attribute via ReLU gating, and not as an associative key-value mapping. We extend these results to the multi-hop setting -- chains of relational queries such as ``Who is the mother of the wife of $x$?'' -- providing constructions with and without chain-of-thought that exhibit a provable capacity-depth tradeoff, complemented by a matching information-theoretic lower bound. Empirically, gradient descent discovers solutions with precisely the predicted structure. Once trained, the MLP transfers zero-shot to entirely new bijections when subject embeddings are appropriately re-initialized, revealing that it has learned a generic selection mechanism rather than memorized any particular set of facts.

</details>


### [198] [A Causal Language Modeling Detour Improves Encoder Continued Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12438)
*Rian Touchent,Eric de la Clergerie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在领域适配中，先用因果语言模型（CLM）训练，再切换到掩码语言模型（MLM）微调的策略，相比直接使用MLM能显著提升下游任务性能。在生物医学文本上，使用ModernBERT在8个法语和11个英语生物医学任务上取得了+0.3~2.8个百分点的提升。研究发现CLM的密集监督主要影响低层Transformer层（0-7层），且这些表示变化在后续MLM阶段得以保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的编码器领域适配方法（继续MLM训练）可能不是最优的，作者希望通过探索CLM+MLM的混合训练策略来提升下游任务表现。

Method: 提出"CLM detour"策略：在领域适配时，先进行一段因果语言模型（CLM）训练，再切换到短时间的掩码语言模型（MLM）训练。使用ModernBERT在生物医学领域进行实验，分析不同层的重要性，并通过冻结实验验证CLM对低层的影响。

Result: 在8个法语和11个英语生物医学任务上，CLM detour策略相比标准MLM基线提升了+0.3~2.8个百分点；CLM的密集监督主要影响低层（0-7层），冻结低层会消除收益，冻结中层则保留收益；表示变化在MLM阶段得以保持。

Conclusion: CLM detour是一种有效的编码器领域适配策略，其收益来源于CLM对低层Transformer的密集监督，这些表示变化在后续MLM阶段得以保留，且收益随模型容量增加而扩大。同时发布了SOTA生物医学编码器ModernCamemBERT-bio和ModernBERT-bio。

Abstract: When adapting an encoder to a new domain, the standard approach is to continue training with Masked Language Modeling (MLM). We show that temporarily switching to Causal Language Modeling (CLM) followed by a short MLM decay improves downstream performance. On biomedical texts with ModernBERT, this CLM detour outperforms MLM baselines trained on identical data and compute across 8 French and 11 English biomedical tasks, by +1.2-2.8pp and +0.3-0.8pp respectively, depending on model size. We investigate the reasons for these gains. We find that CLM's dense supervision impacts low transformer layers (0-7) far more than MLM does. Freezing low layers during CLM eliminates the downstream benefit; freezing mid layers preserves it. The representational changes persist through the MLM decay phase, even when it matches the CLM phase in length, and they scale with model capacity. We release ModernCamemBERT-bio and ModernBERT-bio as state-of-the-art biomedical encoders in Base and Large sizes.

</details>


### [199] [The Algorithmic Caricature: Auditing LLM-Generated Political Discourse Across Crisis Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12452)
*Gunjan,Sidahmed Benabderrahmane,Talal Rahwan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出从计算社会科学视角评估合成政治语篇的群体真实性，通过对比九个危机事件中178万余条真实与合成社交媒体帖子，发现合成语篇虽流畅但在情感、结构、词汇和事件依赖性四个维度上表现出群体层面的不真实性，并提出了"Caricature Gap"指标来量化这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测多聚焦于句子级线索（如困惑度、突发性、标记不规则性），但随着生成模型不断改进，这些信号逐渐减弱。研究旨在从群体层面评估合成政治语篇是否真实反映在线人群的行为特征，弥补传统检测方法的不足。

Method: 构建包含九个危机事件（COVID-19、国会山事件、2020/2024大选、堕胎权案、BLM抗议、中期选举、犹他枪击案、美伊战争）共计1,789,406条帖子的配对语料库，从情感强度、结构规律性、词汇-意识形态框架、跨事件依赖性四个维度，使用均值差距和离散度证据进行对比分析。

Result: 合成语篇在群体层面不真实：情感更消极且离散度更低、结构更规律、词汇更抽象。真实语篇则表现出更广泛的情感变化、更长的尾部结构分布和更具语境特定性的口语化词汇标记。差异程度具有事件依赖性，在快速变化的去中心化危机中更大，在正式或制度中介事件中较小。

Conclusion: 合成政治语篇的主要局限不在于语法或流畅性，而在于群体真实性的降低。群体层面的审计方法可补充传统文本检测，为评估生成语篇的社会真实性提供计算社会科学框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate fluent political text at scale, raising concerns about synthetic discourse during crises and social conflict. Existing AI-text detection often focuses on sentence-level cues such as perplexity, burstiness, or token irregularities, but these signals may weaken as generative systems improve. We instead adopt a Computational Social Science perspective and ask whether synthetic political discourse behaves like an observed online population. We construct a paired corpus of 1,789,406 posts across nine crisis events: COVID-19, the Jan. 6 Capitol attack, the 2020 and 2024 U.S. elections, Dobbs/Roe v. Wade, the 2020 BLM protests, U.S. midterms, the Utah shooting, and the U.S.-Iran war. For each event, we compare observed discourse from social platforms with synthetic discourse generated for the same context. We evaluate four dimensions: emotional intensity, structural regularity, lexical-ideological framing, and cross-event dependency, using mean gaps and dispersion evidence. Across events, synthetic discourse is fluent but population-level unrealistic. It is generally more negative and less dispersed in sentiment, structurally more regular, and lexically more abstract than observed discourse. Observed discourse instead shows broader emotional variation, longer-tailed structural distributions, and more context-specific, colloquial lexical markers. These differences are event-dependent: larger for fast-moving, decentralized crises and smaller for formal or institutionally mediated events. We summarize them with a simple event-level measure, the Caricature Gap. Our findings suggest that the main limitation of synthetic political discourse is not grammar or fluency, but reduced population realism. Population-level auditing complements traditional text-detection and provides a CSS framework for evaluating the social realism of generated discourse.

</details>


### [200] [Task-Adaptive Embedding Refinement via Test-time LLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12487)
*Ariel Gera,Shir Ashury-Tahan,Gal Bloch,Ohad Eytan,Assaf Toledo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM引导的查询精炼范式，通过在大规模文档上利用生成式LLM的反馈实时优化查询的嵌入表示，从而将嵌入模型扩展到零样本搜索和分类任务。实验表明，该方法在多个基准测试中一致提升性能，相对改进最高达+25%，并能更好地反映任务特定的细微约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型在零样本搜索和分类任务中表现有限，而大规模LLM流水线在语料库规模下成本过高，因此需要一种既能提升嵌入模型零样本能力又保持高效率的方法。

Method: 提出LLM引导的查询精炼范式对用户查询的嵌入表示进行优化：利用生成式LLM在一小组文档上提供反馈，实时调整查询嵌入以适应目标任务。该方法结合了LLM的语义理解能力和嵌入模型的计算效率。

Result: 在文献搜索、意图检测、关键点匹配和细粒度指令遵循等任务上相对改进最高达+25%；精炼后的查询提升了排序质量并在语料库中诱导出更清晰的二元分离；所有模型和数据集上均观察到一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法显著扩展了嵌入模型在零样本场景下的实用部署范围，在无法大规模使用成本高昂的LLM流水线时，提供了一种有竞争力的替代方案。

Abstract: We explore the effectiveness of an LLM-guided query refinement paradigm for extending the usability of embedding models to challenging zero-shot search and classification tasks. Our approach refines the embedding representation of a user query using feedback from a generative LLM on a small set of documents, enabling embeddings to adapt in real time to the target task. We conduct extensive experiments with state-of-the-art text embedding models across a diverse set of challenging search and classification benchmarks. Empirical results indicate that LLM-guided query refinement yields consistent gains across all models and datasets, with relative improvements of up to +25% in literature search, intent detection, key-point matching, and nuanced query-instruction following. The refined queries improve ranking quality and induce clearer binary separation across the corpus, enabling the embedding space to better reflect the nuanced, task-specific constraints of each ad-hoc user query. Importantly, this expands the range of practical settings in which embedding models can be effectively deployed, making them a compelling alternative when costly LLM pipelines are not viable at corpus-scale. We release our experimental code for reproducibility, at https://github.com/IBM/task-aware-embedding-refinement.

</details>


### [201] [MultiSoc-4D: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Instruction-Induced Label Collapse in Closed-Set LLM Annotation of Bengali Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06940)
*Souvik Pramanik,S. M. Riaz Rahman Antu,Shak Mohammad Abyad,Md. Ibrahim Khalil,Md. Shahriar Hussain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MultiSoc-4D，一个包含58K+条孟加拉语社交媒体评论的四维度标注基准数据集。通过使用ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude和Grok等LLM进行自动化标注，发现了一种称为"指令诱导标签坍缩"的现象——LLM倾向于选择回退标签（Other/Neutral/No），导致对少数类别的漏检（如79%的仇恨言论和75%的讽刺内容未被检测到），并验证了这是一种"标签一致幻觉"（讽刺检测的Fleiss' Kappa≈-0.001）。跨40+LLM均观察到该标注偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对LLM在低资源语言中遵循封闭集指令行为的系统性分析，特别是自动化标注中的偏差问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建MultiSoc-4D基准数据集，包含58K+条来自六个来源的孟加拉语社交媒体评论，沿类别、情感、仇恨言论和讽刺四个维度标注。采用结构化流水线，由ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude和Grok分别标注不同分区，共享20%验证集，系统诊断LLM标注行为。

Result: 发现"指令诱导标签坍缩"现象：LLM偏好回退标签导致少数类别严重漏检（仇恨79%、讽刺75%）。统计验证了"标签一致幻觉"（讽刺检测Fleiss' Kappa≈-0.001）。跨40+LLM证实该偏差具有普遍性，不受架构差异影响。

Conclusion: MultiSoc-4D可作为孟加拉语NLP中标注偏差的诊断基准，揭示了LLM自动化标注在低资源语言中的系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Annotation automation via Large Language Models (LLMs) is the core approach for scaling NLP datasets; however, LLM behavior with respect to closed-set instructions in low-resource languages has not been well studied. We present MultiSoc-4D, a Bengali social media dataset benchmark, which contains 58K+ social media comments from six sources annotated along four dimensions: category, sentiment, hate speech, and sarcasm. By employing a structured pipeline where ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, and Grok individually annotate separate partitions, while sharing a common validation set of 20%, we diagnose LLM behavior systematically. We discover a prevalent phenomenon called "instruction-induced label collapse", wherein LLMs show a systematic preference towards fallback labels (Other, Neutral, No), leading to high agreement rates but under-detection of minority categories. For example, we find that LLMs failed to detect 79% and 75% of instances with hateful and sarcastic content compared to a human-calibrated reference. Furthermore, we prove that it represents a "label agreement illusion", statistically validated via almost null Fleiss' Kappa ($κ\approx -0.001$) on sarcasm detection. Across 40+ LLMs, we benchmark this annotation bias propagation within the training pipeline, regardless of architectural differences. We release MultiSoc-4D as a diagnostic benchmark for annotation biases in Bengali NLP.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [ECHO: Continuous Hierarchical Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10993)
*Yanbin Hu,Jin Cui,Jiayi Lu,Ruixuan Yang,Jun Ye,Boran Zhao,Xingyu Chen,Xuguang Lan,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ECHO框架，通过双曲自编码器将VLA模型隐藏状态映射到连续分层空间，构建语义记忆树，实现长时域操作任务的高效经验检索与组合泛化，在LIBERO-Long上相比π₀基线提升12.8%绝对成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强架构主要依赖线性或扁平化存储，缺乏操作类别的结构先验与层次化组织，导致长时域任务组合中经验检索效率低、泛化能力受限。受人类经验分层组织启发，旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 提出ECHO框架：1）采用双曲自编码器将VLA隐藏状态映射到连续分层空间；2）利用双曲度量与蕴含约束机制将经验向量组织为语义记忆树，支持自上而下的高效检索；3）设计背景巩固机制，通过几何插值与结构分裂持续优化记忆树，支持连续空间中的虚拟记忆合成。集成到π₀基础模型中。

Result: 在LIBERO-Long上相比π₀基线实现12.8%的绝对成功率提升；在跨套件的未见长时域任务上提升了组合泛化能力；初步真实世界实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: ECHO通过双曲空间的层次化记忆组织有效提升了VLA模型在长时域操作任务中的表现，验证了结构先验对经验检索与组合泛化的重要性。

Abstract: Memory capacity is a critical factor determining the performance of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in long-horizon manipulation tasks. Existing memory-augmented architectures primarily rely on linear or flat storage, lacking structural priors for manipulation categories and hierarchical organization. This deficiency hinders efficient experience retrieval and limits generalization to unseen long-horizon task compositions. Inspired by the hierarchical organization of human experience, we propose ECHO (Experience Consolidation and Hierarchical Organization), a novel memory framework operating within a Continuous Hierarchical Space. By employing a hyperbolic autoencoder, ECHO maps VLA hidden states into this space. Leveraging hyperbolic metrics and entailment constraint mechanisms, experience vectors are organized into a semantic memory tree that supports efficient top-down retrieval. In parallel, a background consolidation mechanism continuously refines the memory tree through geometric interpolation and structural splitting, supporting virtual memory synthesis in the continuous space. We integrate ECHO into the $π_0$ foundation model. Evaluations on LIBERO and preliminary real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, notably achieving a 12.8% absolute improvement in execution success rate over the $π_0$ baseline on LIBERO-Long, while improving compositional generalization on cross-suite unseen long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [203] [ForceFlow: Learning to Feel and Act via Contact-Driven Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11048)
*Shuoheng Zhang,Yifu Yuan,Hongyao Tang,Yan Zheng,Qiaojun Yu,Pengyi Li,Guowei Huang,Helong Huang,Xingyue Quan,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceFlow 是基于流匹配的力感知反应式框架，采用非对称多模态融合架构将力信号作为全局调节信号，并结合视觉-触觉两阶段分层分解（VLM指向+力驱动执行），在六类真实接触密集任务上相比 ForceVLA 提升 37% 的成功率，同时成本更低，具备零样本 OOD 泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法在接触密集操作任务中面临复杂接触动力学挑战，亟需高精度力反馈与控制；尽管已有工作尝试将力/力矩传感融入策略，但如何构建简单有效的框架以实现多模态观测下的鲁棒泛化仍是开放问题。

Method: 1）采用非对称多模态融合架构，将力信号作为全局调节信号，结合联合预测范式实现力与运动的深度耦合；2）任务级分层分解为视觉主导的趋近阶段（VLM 指向定位）和触觉主导的交互阶段（力驱动接触执行），并通过 Vision-to-Force (V2F) 切换机制显式解耦空间泛化与接触调控；3）基于流匹配（flow matching）构建力感知反应式策略。

Result: 在六类真实接触密集任务上，ForceFlow 相比强基线 ForceVLA 成功率提升 37%，成本显著降低；具备精确的力信号预测能力、接触力自调节能力和零样本 OOD 泛化能力。

Conclusion: ForceFlow 通过力-运动深度耦合与视觉-触觉分层分解，有效解决了接触密集操作中的力反馈与控制难题，实现了低成本、高泛化的鲁棒操作性能。

Abstract: Existing imitation learning methods enable robots to interact autonomously with the physical environment. However, contact-rich manipulation tasks remain a significant challenge due to complex contact dynamics that demand high-precision force feedback and control. Although recent efforts have attempted to integrate force/torque sensing into policies, how to build a simple yet effective framework that achieves robust generalization under multimodal observations remains an open question. In this paper, we propose ForceFlow, a force-aware reactive framework built upon flow matching. For contact-stage policy design, we investigate force signal fusion mechanisms and adopt an asymmetric multimodal fusion architecture that treats force as a global regulatory signal, combined with a joint prediction paradigm that enhances the policy's understanding of instantaneous force and historical information, thereby achieving deep coupling between force and motion. For task-level hierarchical decomposition, we divide manipulation into a vision-dominant approach stage (VLM-based pointing for target localization) and a touch-dominant interaction stage (force-driven contact execution), with a Vision-to-Force (V2F) handover mechanism that explicitly decouples spatial generalization from contact regulation. Experimental results across six real-world contact-rich tasks demonstrate that ForceFlow achieves a 37% success rate improvement over the strong baseline ForceVLA while maintaining significantly lower cost. Moreover, ForceFlow exhibits accurate force signal prediction and demonstrates superior performance in contact force self-regulation and zero-shot out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization.

</details>


### [204] [SEVO: Semantic-Enhanced Virtual Observation for Robust VLA Manipulation via Active Illumination and Data-Centric Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11114)
*Tianchonghui Fang,Yuan Zhuang,Fei Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SEVO（语义增强虚拟观测）数据驱动方法，通过体固定相机、红光照明、YOLO语义分割叠加和多样化数据采集协议，在不改变策略架构的情况下，将低成本机器人操作策略在新环境中的成功率从30-35%提升至75-85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA和模仿学习策略在训练环境外部署时频繁失败，社区实践者报告跨环境迁移性能几乎为零，而现有基准测试仅在受控固定背景下报告高成功率，存在严重泛化鸿沟。

Method: SEVO包含三个观测改造机制：(1)体固定相机覆盖完整操作空间；(2)主动红光照明归一化物体外观；(3)实时YOLO分割叠加提供背景不变语义线索。关键要素是多样化数据采集协议（系统化改变光照、背景和干扰物）。

Result: 完整流程在训练环境达到ACT 95%/SmolVLA 83%抓取成功率，新环境迁移后保持85%/75%；无SEVO时训练环境仅75%/70%，新环境崩溃至30-35%。

Conclusion: 原则性的观测设计和数据采集多样性（而非模型规模扩展）是实现低成本机器人在日常家庭环境可靠运行的关键。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and imitation-learning policies trained via community toolchains on low-cost hardware frequently fail when deployed outside the training environment. Existing evaluations, including the original ACT and SmolVLA benchmarks, demonstrate high success rates under controlled, fixed backgrounds, yet community practitioners report near-zero transfer to new environments. We present SEVO (Semantic-Enhanced Virtual Observation), a data-centric approach that improves cross-environment manipulation robustness without modifying the policy architecture. SEVO transforms the raw RGB camera stream through three mechanisms: (1) body-fixed cameras whose combined fields of view cover the full manipulation workspace, (2) active red-spectrum illumination that physically normalizes object appearance, and (3) real-time YOLO segmentation overlay that provides a background-invariant semantic cue. Critically, we show that a diversified data collection protocol (systematically varying lighting, backgrounds, and distractors during teleoperation) is the single most important factor for generalization. We target transparent water bottles, objects that visually blend with their surroundings, and select a simple pick-and-place task to enable hundreds of controlled real-robot trials across two mobile platforms. The full pipeline achieves 95% grasp success with ACT and 83% with SmolVLA in the training environment, transferring to novel environments at 85% and 75%. Without SEVO, the same policies achieve only 75%/70% in training and collapse to 30-35% in novel environments. Our results demonstrate that principled observation design and environmental diversity during data collection, not model scaling, enable low-cost robots to operate reliably in everyday household environments.

</details>


### [205] [ASIP-Planner: Adaptive Planning for UAV Surface Inspection in Partially Known Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11119)
*Hanyu Jin,Zhefan Xu,Haoyu Shen,Xinming Han,Kanlong Ye,Kenji Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应无人机检测框架，用于部分已知的结构化室内环境。该框架结合基于分段的全局覆盖规划器和面向检测的局部视角自适应模块，通过表面对齐聚类生成视角序列，并在线调整观测方向以减轻遮挡导致的覆盖损失。仿真和实物实验表明，该方法在保证近全覆盖的同时减少了轨迹长度，提升了检测效率和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有覆盖规划方法假设环境完全已知并基于精确先验地图进行确定性全局视点优化，但室内检测中使用的楼层平面图可能未反映临时结构或设备等不可预见障碍，导致视点遮挡和检测质量下降。需一种能适应环境差异的检测框架。

Method: 提出自适应无人机检测框架：1) 全局规划器将平面检测目标按表面对齐聚类，生成紧凑且朝向一致性更好的视点序列；2) 局部规划器在线生成无碰撞轨迹并调整观测方向，在保持规划轨迹结构的同时减少遮挡导致的覆盖损失。

Result: 仿真实验表明全局规划器在随机场景中实现近全覆盖，轨迹长度优于基线方法。真实飞行实验验证框架可生成可用于下游分析的检测数据。

Conclusion: 该框架在部分已知的结构化室内环境中提升了检测效率和适应性，有效应对先验地图与环境实际差异带来的遮挡问题。

Abstract: Indoor infrastructure inspection, such as tunnels and industrial facilities, requires systematic surface coverage to ensure that all inspection targets are properly observed. Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offer an alternative to manual inspection by conducting map-guided surface inspection using prior structural models. However, in practice, indoor inspection often relies on floorplan-derived reference maps that may not reflect unforeseen obstacles, such as temporary structures or equipment, leading to occluded viewpoints and degraded inspection quality. Existing coverage planning methods typically assume a fully known inspection environment and perform deterministic global viewpoint optimization based on accurate prior maps, making them vulnerable to environmental discrepancies during execution. This work presents an adaptive UAV inspection framework for partially known structured indoor environments. The proposed method integrates a segment-based global coverage planner with an inspection-oriented local view-angle adaptation module. The global planner organizes planar inspection targets into surface-aligned clusters to generate compact viewpoint sequences with improved orientation consistency. The local planner generates collision-free trajectories and adjusts the viewing direction online to mitigate occlusion-induced coverage loss while preserving the planned trajectory structure. The simulation results across randomized scene configurations demonstrate that the proposed global planner achieves near-complete coverage while reducing trajectory length compared to representative baselines. Real-world flight experiments further validate that the framework produces usable inspection data for downstream analysis. These results indicate that the proposed framework improves inspection efficiency and adaptability in partially known structured indoor environments.

</details>


### [206] [Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting for Predictive 3D Representation in Language-Guided Pick-and-Place Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11144)
*Kaixin Jia,Jiacheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Forecast-GS的预测性3D表示框架，通过显式建模任务完成状态来改善语言引导的机器人操作，在真实世界的抓取放置任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作系统通常仅基于当前场景推理，缺乏对任务完成状态的显式建模，导致在需要满足空间和语义目标的局部观测条件下难以评估候选动作的可行性。

Method: 提出Forecast-GS框架，将语言指令与3D高斯泼溅表示结合，显式预测任务完成的3D状态，从而实现更可靠的动作评估。

Result: 在Cutter-to-Box、Apple-to-Bowl和Sponge-to-Tray三个真实操作任务上，自动模式成功率分别为21/25、23/25和16/25，优于ReKep基线（15/25、19/25、10/25）；人工辅助模式进一步提升至23/25、24/25和19/25。

Conclusion: 显式预测任务完成3D状态能提升动作评估可靠性，但最终状态自动排序仍存在不足，是未来全自主操作的重要挑战。

Abstract: We introduce Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting (Forecast-GS), a predictive 3D representation framework for language-conditioned robotic manipulation. While recent manipulation systems have made progress by grounding language instructions into robot affordances, value maps, or relational keypoint constraints, they usually reason over the current scene and do not explicitly model the task-completed state. This limitation is critical when success depends on satisfying spatial and semantic goals under partial observations, where the robot must evaluate whether a candidate action leads to a feasible task-consistent outcome.
  We validate Forecast-GS on real-world pick-and-place manipulation tasks, including Cutter-to-Box, Apple-to-Bowl, and Sponge-to-Tray. For each task, we conduct 25 real-world trials under varied initial object configurations using the same robot platform and sensing setup. Forecast-GS with automatic candidate selection achieves success rates of 21/25, 23/25, and 16/25 on the three tasks, respectively, outperforming the ReKep baseline, which achieves 15/25, 19/25, and 10/25. A diagnostic human-assisted setting further improves success rates to 23/25, 24/25, and 19/25, suggesting that candidate generation is effective while automatic ranking remains imperfect. These results suggest that explicitly forecasting task-completed 3D states enables more reliable action evaluation, while the gap between automatic and human-assisted selection indicates that robust final-state ranking remains an important challenge for fully autonomous manipulation. Overall, Forecast-GS provides an interpretable bridge between language understanding, 3D perception, and robotic manipulation planning.

</details>


### [207] [Distributed Pose Graph Optimization via Continuous Riemannian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11210)
*Jaeho Shin,Maani Ghaffari,Yulun Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于李群上二阶连续时间动力学的分布式位姿图优化框架，将位姿变量建模为受阻尼的质点，通过阻尼欧拉-庞加莱方程和半隐式几何积分器设计优化算法，在同步和异步通信下均优于现有分布式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式位姿图优化方法在处理多机器人系统中的通信延迟、收敛速度和计算效率方面存在不足，需要一种理论上严谨且实用的分布式优化框架。

Method: 将PGO问题形式化为李群上的二阶连续时间动力学系统（阻尼欧拉-庞加莱方程），位姿变量作为受阻尼的质点；采用半隐式几何积分器进行离散化；在分布式场景中使用块对角质量和阻尼矩阵，每个机器人独立求解ODE；利用状态和速度建模实现邻居预测以应对通信延迟。

Result: 在基准PGO数据集上，所提方法在同步和异步通信模式下均优于现有的分布式基线方法，收敛速度更快且精度更高。

Conclusion: 基于李群二阶动力学的PGO框架有效统一并推广了Riemannian梯度下降和高斯-牛顿等经典方法，理论分析保证了能量耗散，分布式变体在多机器人场景下具有低通信开销和良好的延迟鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present a framework for distributed Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) by formulating the problem as a second-order continuous-time dynamical system evolving on Lie groups. By modeling pose variables as massive particles subject to damping, the equilibrium points of the resulting Riemannian dynamics coincide with first-order critical points of the original PGO problem. Using the governing damped Euler--Poincaré equations and a semi-implicit geometric integrator, we design an optimization algorithm that generalizes existing algorithms such as Riemannian gradient descent and Gauss--Newton. In multi-robot settings, we present a fully distributed and parallel method based on block-diagonal mass and damping matrices, where each robot solves an ordinary differential equation for its own poses with minimal communication overhead. Moreover, modeling both state and velocity enables principled neighbor prediction that significantly improves convergence under delayed communication. Theoretically, we present an analysis and establish sufficient condition that ensures energy dissipation under the employed geometric discretization scheme. Experiments on benchmark PGO datasets demonstrate that the proposed solver achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art distributed baselines in both synchronous and asynchronous regimes.

</details>


### [208] [Computational Design of a Low-Visibility UAV Using a Human-Aligned Perceptual Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11296)
*Jingxian Wang,Chen Yu,David Matthews,Emma Alexander,Sam Kriegman,Michael Rubenstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Phantom Twist——一种通过高速旋转和运动模糊实现低可见性的单螺旋桨无人机，并开发了两阶段自动化设计流水线，在满足惯性及气动约束的前提下优化组件布局以最小化视觉感知度。通过制造和飞行测试验证了设计的稳定性和低可感知性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多旋翼无人机（如四旋翼）在视觉上容易被察觉，限制了其在隐秘侦察等场景下的应用。作者希望通过高速旋转产生运动模糊来降低无人机的视觉显著性，从而设计一种低可见性的单螺旋桨无人机。

Method: 提出一个两阶段自动化设计流水线：第一阶段优化功能性组件（电池、控制PCB、电机-螺旋桨组件、配重块）的布局，以最小化基于人类感知的度量LPIPS（Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity）为指标的可见性；第二阶段严格满足惯性（质心、转动惯量）和气动约束，确保稳定飞行的可行性。最终通过制造和实际飞行测试进行验证。

Result: 制造并测试了多个原型，证实设计流水线能产生稳定可控的无人机，且优化后的Phantom Twist相比传统四旋翼无人机在视觉可感知性上显著降低。

Conclusion: 通过高速旋转利用运动模糊可以有效降低无人机的视觉显著性；所提出的自动化设计流水线能够在满足飞行约束的前提下有效优化低可见性设计；该思路为未来低可探测性无人机的设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We introduce Phantom Twist, a type of single-propeller UAV designed to achieve low visibility through high-speed spinning and the exploitation of motion blur. We develop a two-stage automated design pipeline that optimizes the placement of functional components including batteries, control PCB, motor-propeller assembly, and counterweights. The pipeline minimizes visibility as measured by a human-aligned perceptual metric (LPIPS) while strictly satisfying inertial and aerodynamic constraints required for stable flight. We validate this approach through fabrication and flight testing of multiple prototypes. These tests confirm that our pipeline produces stable, controllable designs and that the optimized UAV exhibits significantly reduced visual perceptibility compared to conventional quadcopters.

</details>


### [209] [Kairos: A Scalable Serving System for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11381)
*Yinwei Dai,Ganesh Ananthanarayanan,Landon Cox,Xenofon Foukas,Bozidar Radunovic,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kairos是首个专为物理AI设计的多机器人服务系统，通过将"生成-执行循环"作为一等公民并主动参与执行阶段，相比数字AI服务方法降低31.8-66.5%的端到端任务延迟，且收益随机器人集群规模扩大而增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI任务的推理特性与数字AI显著不同（多轮推理与执行交错、生成动作块、异步交织），导致现有数字AI服务系统无法适配物理AI场景，这严重阻碍了大规模机器人集群的广泛部署。

Method: Kairos将"生成-执行循环"（generate-execute loop）提升为一等公民，在系统设计中主动参与执行阶段，实现推理与执行的高效异步交织，并针对多机器人集群进行优化调度。

Result: 在多种物理AI模型和机器人平台上，Kairos相比最先进的数字AI服务方法将平均端到端任务延迟降低31.8-66.5%，且性能增益随机器人集群规模扩大而持续提升。

Conclusion: 物理AI需要专门设计的服务系统来匹配其独特的推理-执行模式，Kairos证明了将执行阶段纳入系统主动管理能够显著提升多机器人任务效率，为物理AI的大规模部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Physical AI is experiencing rapid growth with frontier foundation models increasing its capabilities across general environments. Physical AI tasks are characterized by inference properties that are markedly different from digital AI. They consist of multiple rounds of inference and action execution, generating a chunk of actions in each inference round, and asynchronously interleaving inference and execution. This makes existing digital AI serving systems unsuited for physical AI; a shortcoming that is critical for enabling their wide adoption, considering their size and the scale of the robot fleets they have to serve. To fill this gap, we design Kairos, the first multi-robot serving system that makes the generate-execute loop a first-class citizen, with active involvement in the execution phase. Across a wide range of physical AI models and robots, Kairos reduces the average end-to-end task latency by 31.8--66.5% over state-of-the-art digital AI serving practices, with gains scaling with the robot fleet size.

</details>


### [210] [Overcoming Dynamics-Blindness: Training-Free Pace-and-Path Correction for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11459)
*Yanyan Zhang,Chaoda Song,Vikash Singh,Xinpeng Li,Kai Ye,Zhe Hu,Zhongzhu Pu,Yu Yin,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Pace-and-Path Correction，一种无需训练的闭式推理时算子，通过正交分解为节奏通道和路径通道来校正VLA模型在动态环境中的动作执行，在MoveBench基准上相比基线方法提升高达28.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型多基于单帧观测训练，对时间动态结构性地盲视，在非平稳场景中性能严重退化。现有方法要么需要昂贵重训练，要么存在延迟瓶颈和动作块间时间一致性差的问题。

Method: 提出Pace-and-Path Correction，一个无需训练的闭式推理时算子。从单一二次代价函数出发，联合最小化得到统一解，正交分解为两个通道：节奏通道沿计划方向压缩执行，路径通道施加正交空间偏移，共同吸收块窗口内的感知动态。

Result: 在MoveBench诊断基准上，该方法在动态-only环境中相比基础VLA模型绝对成功率提升28.8%，在静态-动态混合环境中提升25.9%，持续优于SOTA的无需训练包装器和动态自适应方法。

Conclusion: Pace-and-Path Correction是一种高效的训练无关推理时校正方法，能够显著提升VLA模型在动态环境中的鲁棒性和时间一致性，且无需重新训练模型。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve remarkable flexibility and generalization beyond classical control paradigms. However, most prevailing VLAs are trained under a single-frame observation paradigm, which leaves them structurally blind to temporal dynamics. Consequently, these models degrade severely in non-stationary scenarios, even when trained or finetuned on dynamic datasets. Existing approaches either require expensive retraining or suffer from latency bottlenecks and poor temporal consistency across action chunks. We propose Pace-and-Path Correction, a training-free, closed-form inference-time operator that wraps any chunked-action VLA. From a single quadratic cost, joint minimization yields a unified solution that decomposes orthogonally into two distinct channels. The pace channel compresses execution along the planned direction, while the path channel applies an orthogonal spatial offset, jointly absorbing the perceived dynamics within the chunk window. We evaluate our approach on a comprehensive diagnostic benchmark MoveBench designed to isolate motion as the sole controlled variable. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-free wrappers and dynamic-adaptive methods and improves success rates by up to 28.8% and 25.9% in absolute terms over foundational VLA models in dynamic-only and static-dynamic mixed environments, respectively.

</details>


### [211] [Offline Policy Evaluation for Manipulation Policies via Discounted Liveness Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11479)
*Hao Wang,Joshua Bowden,Colton Crosby,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于活性的Bellman算子框架，用于从稀疏奖励中进行离线策略评估，该框架将策略评估视为任务完成问题，产生对有限截断鲁棒的保守不动点值函数，在模拟和真实操作任务中优于TD(0)和蒙特卡洛方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代操作系统中策略评估面临稀疏奖励、任务进展非单调以及有限长度评估轨迹带来的截断偏差问题，打破了依赖Bellman方程/最优性原理的标准方法的无限时域假设。

Method: 提出基于活性(liveness)的Bellman算子框架，将策略评估形式化为任务完成问题，生成保守的不动点值函数，对有限时域截断具有鲁棒性；分析了算子包括收缩保证在内的理论性质。

Result: 在两项模拟操作任务（使用VLA模型和扩散策略）以及一项布料折叠任务（使用人类演示）中，该方法更准确反映任务进展并显著减少截断偏差，优于TD(0)和蒙特卡洛策略评估等经典基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于活性Bellman算子的离线策略评估框架有效解决了稀疏奖励下的截断偏差问题，为机器人操作策略评估提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Policy evaluation is a fundamental component of the development and deployment pipeline for robotic policies. In modern manipulation systems, this problem is particularly challenging: rewards are often sparse, task progression of evaluation rollouts are often non-monotonic as the policies exhibit recovery behaviors, and evaluation rollouts are necessarily of finite length. This finite length introduces truncation bias, breaking the infinite-horizon assumptions underlying standard methods relying on Bellman equations/principle of optimality. In this work, we propose a framework for offline policy evaluation from sparse rewards based on a liveness-based Bellman operator. Our formulation interprets policy evaluation as a task-completion problem and yields a conservative fixed-point value function that is robust to finite-horizon truncation. We analyze the theoretical properties of the proposed operator, including contraction guarantees, and show how it encodes task progression while mitigating truncation bias. We evaluate our method on two simulated manipulation tasks using both a Vision-Language-Action model and a diffusion policy, and a cloth folding task using human demonstrations. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach more accurately reflects task progress and substantially reduces truncation bias, outperforming classical baselines such as TD(0) and Monte Carlo policy evaluation.

</details>


### [212] [Coordinated Diffusion: Generating Multi-Agent Behavior Without Multi-Agent Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11485)
*Lasse Peters,Laura Ferranti,Javier Alonso-Mora,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDi框架，通过耦合独立训练的单个智能体扩散策略和用户定义的多智能体成本函数，在不依赖协调演示的情况下学习多智能体策略，实现了从单智能体数据到多智能体协调行为的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体模仿学习面临数据瓶颈：联合状态-动作空间随智能体数量指数增长，收集协调的多智能体演示成本极高。作者希望利用更容易获取的单智能体演示数据来学习多智能体协调策略。

Method: 提出Coordinated Diffusion (CoDi)框架，将独立预训练的单个智能体扩散策略通过用户定义的多智能体成本函数进行耦合。扩散得分函数分解为独立的单智能体预训练基策略加上一个协调指导项，该指导项以无梯度方式估计，适用于黑箱、不可微的成本函数。

Result: 在双臂操作任务的仿真和硬件实验中，CoDi能从单智能体数据中发现鲁棒的协调行为，比多智能体基线方法更高效，验证了联合指导、基策略支持和成本设计的重要性。

Conclusion: 演示数据与成本函数发挥互补作用：单智能体演示需覆盖目标多智能体行为的支撑集，成本函数需从单智能体策略的乘积空间中促进期望行为。该方法无需协调演示即可实现有效的多智能体协调。

Abstract: Imitation learning powered by generative models has proven effective for modeling complex single-agent behaviors. However, teaching multi-agent systems, like multiple arms or vehicles, to coordinate through imitation learning is hindered by a fundamental data bottleneck: as the joint state-action space grows exponentially with the number of agents, collecting a sufficient amount of coordinated multi-agent demonstrations becomes extremely costly. In this work, we ask: how can we leverage single-agent demonstration data to learn multi-agent policies? We present Coordinated Diffusion (CoDi), a framework that couples independently trained single-agent diffusion policies through a user-defined multi-agent cost function, without requiring any coordinated demonstrations. We derive a new diffusion-based sampling scheme wherein the diffusion score function decomposes into independent, single-agent pre-trained base policies plus a cost-driven guidance term that coordinates these base policies into cohesive multi-agent behavior. We show that this guidance term can be estimated in a gradient-free manner, making CoDi applicable to black-box, non-differentiable cost functions without additional training. Theoretically and empirically, we analyze the conditions under which this composition can faithfully approximate a target multi-agent behavior. We find a complementary role for demonstration data versus the cost function: single-agent demonstrations must cover the support of the desired multi-agent behavior, while the cost function must promote desired behavior from this product of single-agent policies. Our results in simulation and hardware experiments of a two-arm manipulation task show that CoDi discovers robust coordinated behavior from single-agent data, is more data-efficient than multi-agent baselines, and highlights the importance of joint guidance, base policy support, and cost design.

</details>


### [213] [RIO: Flexible Real-Time Robot I/O for Cross-Embodiment Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11564)
*Pablo Ortega-Kral,Eliot Xing,Arthur Bucker,Vernon Luk,Junseo Kim,Owen Kwon,Angchen Xie,Nikhil Sobanbabu,Yifu Yuan,Megan Lee,Deepam Ameria,Bhaswanth Ayapilla,Jaycie Bussell,Guanya Shi,Jonathan Francis,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RIO（Robot I/O），一个开源的Python框架，提供灵活的轻量级组件用于机器人控制、遥操作、数据格式化、传感器配置和策略部署，支持多种硬件平台和形态。通过在三种形态（单臂、双臂、人形）和四个硬件平台上验证，并使用RIO收集的遥操作数据微调π0.5和GR00T等VLA模型，展示了跨形态通用机器人能力的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前跨形态通用机器人能力的发展受到碎片化基础设施的限制：大多数机器人代码高度特定于用户所决定的具体设置，导致在尝试重用、回收或共享构件时产生大量额外开销。

Method: 开发了RIO框架，提供抽象接口使得用户能够进行任意选择并在不同配置间轻松切换，实现机器人的控制、遥操作、数据格式化、传感器配置和策略部署等功能。

Result: 在三种形态（单臂、双臂、人形）和四个硬件平台（不同夹爪和相机配置）上验证了VLA部署工作流；使用RIO收集的遥操作数据成功微调了π0.5和GR00T等最先进VLA模型，在家庭任务（抓取放置、折叠、擦洗碗碟）上取得良好效果。

Conclusion: 通过开源RIO框架，希望能够加速机器人学习社区在真实机器人硬件上的研究进度，促进跨形态通用机器人能力的实现。

Abstract: Despite recent efforts to collect multi-task, multi-embodiment datasets, to design recipes for training Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs), and to showcase these models on different robot platforms, generalist cross-embodiment robot capabilities remains a largely elusive ideal. Progress is limited by fragmented infrastructure: most robot code is highly specific to the exact setup the user decided on, which adds major overhead when attempting to reuse, recycle, or share artifacts between users. We present RIO (Robot I/O), an open source Python framework that provides flexible, lightweight components for robot control, teleoperation, data formatting, sensor configuration, and policy deployment across diverse hardware platforms and morphologies. RIO provides abstractions that enable users to make any choice and to switch between them, with minimal reconfiguration effort. We validate RIO on VLA deployment workflows across three morphologies (single-arm, bimanual, humanoid) and four hardware platforms with varying grippers and cameras. Using teleoperated data collected with RIO, we fine-tune state-of-the-art VLAs including $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T on household tasks such as pick-and-place, folding, and bowl scrubbing. By open sourcing all our efforts, we hope the community can accelerate their pace of robot learning on real-world robot hardware. Additional details at: https://robot-i-o.github.io

</details>


### [214] [Sampling-Based Follow-the-Leader Motion Planning for Manipulator-Mounted Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11618)
*Chengnan Shentu,Nicholas Baldassini,Oluwagbotemi D. Iseoluwa,Radian Gondokaryono,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对安装在机械臂上的连续体机器人的跟随者运动规划方法，通过解耦全局形状搜索与基座姿态计算，实现高效在线规划，在120条模拟路径上达到100%成功率和0%尖端误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FTL方法通常假设固定基座或单自由度插入机构，限制了在机械臂安装CR实际系统中的应用。

Method: 将全局形状搜索与基座姿态确定解耦，通过闭式几何构造计算基座姿态，避免在线迭代优化；将大部分计算移至离线阶段，支持通用前向模型。

Result: 在3类测试的120条模拟路径上，实现0%尖端误差、1.9%平均形状偏差（相对于机器人长度）、100%成功率，并在6-DOF腱驱动CR安装于串联机械臂上验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法具有分辨率完备的形状搜索和收敛的尖端跟踪理论保证，有效解决了机械臂安装CR的FTL运动规划问题。

Abstract: Follow-the-leader (FTL) motion exploits the unique morphology of continuum robots (CRs) to navigate confined spaces by having the body retrace the path of the tip. While extensively studied, existing FTL methods typically assume a fixed base or a single degree-of-freedom insertion mechanism, limiting their applicability to practical systems in which CRs are mounted on robotic manipulators with fully actuated SE(3) base pose. This paper presents a sampling-based motion planner for FTL motion of manipulator-mounted CRs that jointly considers robot configuration and base pose. The key idea is to decouple global shape search from base pose determination by computing the base pose through a closed-form geometric construction, thereby avoiding iterative optimization during online planning. The approach supports general forward models and enables efficient planning by shifting the majority of computation offline. We establish theoretical guarantees including resolution complete shape search and converging tip tracking throughout waypoint traversal and interpolation. Experiments on 120 simulated paths over 3 test classes demonstrate 0% tip error and 1.9% mean shape deviation (w.r.t. robot length) at 100% success rate. We validate the practicality of our approach on a 6-DOF tendon-driven CR mounted on a serial manipulator. Code and visualization available at https://continuumroboticslab.github.io/sb-ftl-cr-planner/.

</details>


### [215] [A Proprioceptive-Only Benchmark for Quadruped State Estimation: ATE, RPE, and Runtime Trade-offs Between Filters and Smoothers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11674)
*Ylenia Nisticò,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Joan Solà,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在GrandTour数据集上对比了三种四足机器人本体状态估计器（MUSE、IEKF、IS），报告了ATE、RPE和计算时间，发现IEKF和IS的ATE优于MUSE，而RPE相近，并开源了所有评估代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为四足机器人领域从业者提供关于三种主流本体状态估计器在精度与计算时间上的清晰对比指导，帮助其根据应用约束选择合适的估计器。

Method: 在固定硬件/软件栈上，使用GrandTour数据集的CYN-1序列，以ATE（长期精度）、平移和旋转RPE（短期精度）及每次更新的计算时间为指标，对MUSE、IEKF和IS三种方法进行公平比较。

Result: 三种方法的RPE大致相似；IEKF和IS的ATE低于MUSE；运行时间结果揭示了三种方法在精度与延迟之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 本研究为从业者提供了关于精度与计算成本的简明评估快照，并开源了全部代码以确保可复现性，有助于读者根据应用需求选择合适的状态估计器。

Abstract: We compare three state-of-the-art proprioceptive state estimators for quadruped robots: MUSE [1], the Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF) [2], and the Invariant Smoother (IS) [3], on the CYN-1 sequence of the GrandTour Dataset [4]. Our goal is to give practitioners clear guidance on accuracy and computation time: we report long-term accuracy (Absolute Trajectory Error, ATE), short-term accuracy (translational and rotational Relative Pose Error, RPE), and per-update computation time on a fixed hardware/software stack. On this dataset, RPEs are broadly similar across methods, while IEKF and IS achieve a lower ATE than MUSE. Runtime results highlight the accuracy-latency trade-offs across the three approaches. In the discussion, we outline the evaluation choices used to ensure a fair comparison and analyze factors that influence short-horizon metrics. Overall, this study provides a concise snapshot of accuracy and cost, helping readers choose an estimator that fits their application constraints, with all evaluation code and documentation released open-source at https://github.com/iit-DLSLab/state_estimation_benchmark for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [216] [Rainbow Deep Q-Learning with Kinematics-Aware Design for Cooperative Delta and 3-RRS Parallel Robot Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11697)
*Hassen Nigatu,Gaokun Shi,Jituo Li,Wang Jin,Lu Guodong*

Main category: cs.RO
