TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Rainbow DQN的运动学感知深度强化学习框架，用于Delta并联机器人与3-RRS并联机构的协作插销入孔任务。通过几何设计优化扩大无奇异工作空间，将问题建模为12维状态、12离散动作的MDP，采用两阶段课程训练，在仿真中实现了优于传统DQN和采样规划器的稳定策略收敛与可靠装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决并联机器人协作装配中存在的奇异点约束和工作空间限制问题，通过运动学感知的几何优化与深度强化学习结合，提升插销入孔任务的成功率与安全性。

Method: 1) 几何设计优化阶段：调整3-RRS并联机构几何参数以最大化无奇异工作空间；2) 将协作插销入孔建模为MDP，状态向量12维，离散动作集12个；3) 采用Rainbow DQN（集成Double DQN、Dueling架构、优先经验回放、多步回报、噪声线性层、分布式价值头）；4) 两阶段课程训练策略。

Result: 在高保真运动学仿真中，该方法实现了稳定的策略收敛、可靠的插销插入性能，并相比普通DQN和经典采样规划器显著减少了违反约束的次数。

Conclusion: 运动学感知的几何设计优化与Rainbow DQN的协同设计框架有效解决了并联机器人协作装配中的奇异点和工作空间约束问题，为高精度工业装配提供了可行的强化学习解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a kinematics-aware deep reinforcement learning framework based on Rainbow Deep Q-Networks (DQN) for cooperative peg-in-hole manipulation by a Delta parallel robot and a 3-RRS (Revolute--Revolute--Spherical) parallel manipulator. A key contribution is the integration of a geometric design-optimization stage that precedes learning: the 3-RRS geometry is tuned to maximize the singularity-free workspace and improve conditioning, which in turn enlarges the safe region in which the reinforcement learning policy can explore. Together the two manipulators expose a 6~degree-of-freedom (DoF) controllable subspace (three Delta translations, two 3-RRS rotations, and one 3-RRS vertical translation); the peg-in-hole task is invariant to rotation about the peg axis, so the task-relevant manifold is five dimensional. The cooperative insertion problem is cast as a Markov Decision Process with a 12-dimensional state vector and a discrete action set containing $6 \times 2 = 12$ incremental commands (one positive and one negative per controlled DoF). A shaped reward combines dense proximity guidance, penalties for kinematic and workspace violations, and sparse bonuses for successful insertions. The Rainbow DQN -- integrating double Q-learning, dueling architecture, prioritized replay, multi-step returns, noisy linear layers for exploration, and a distributional value head -- is trained with a two-stage curriculum. The co-designed framework is validated in a high-fidelity kinematic simulator, where it achieves stable policy convergence, reliable insertions, and reduced constraint violations compared against a vanilla DQN agent and a classical sampling-based planner.

</details>


### [217] [Introducing Environmental Constraints to Grasping Strategies for Paper-Like Flexible Materials Using a Soft Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11714)
*Yi Dong,Yang Li,Jinjun Duan,Zhendong Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对类纸材料（对压缩应力敏感、物理特性微小变化即可显著影响抓取）这一特定柔性物体，利用通用软体抓手与环境约束，系统研究了抓取策略。基于现有操控基元提出了利用环境约束的系统性抓取策略，并分析了力学与运动学模型。建立了抓取力与成功率的评价体系进行实验验证，最后总结了不同策略的工作空间与特性，为家用服务机器人抓取平面柔性物体提供应用参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类纸材料在工业与服务场景中广泛存在，但其对压缩应力敏感，物理特性微小变化会显著影响抓取效果，现有针对布料等柔性物体的抓取策略不完全适用，需要针对类纸材料的特性进行系统研究。

Method: 基于现有抓取策略中的操控基元，提出利用环境约束的系统性抓取策略；分析各策略的力学与运动学模型；定义抓取力与成功率的评价体系进行实验评估。

Result: 建立了针对类纸材料的系统性抓取策略框架，通过实验评估了不同材料与工况对抓取力和成功率的影响，并总结了各策略的工作空间与特性。

Conclusion: 所提出的利用环境约束的抓取策略可满足不同任务需求，为家用服务机器人抓取平面柔性物体提供了可行的应用方案。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of flexible objects is widely required in both industrial and service applications. Among such objects, paper-like materials exhibit distinct mechanical characteristics compared to cloth, being more sensitive to compressive stress, where minor variations in physical properties can significantly affect grasping. This study systematically investigates grasping strategies for paper-like materials using a universal soft gripper by exploiting environmental constraints. Based on manipulation primitives employed in existing grasping strategies, we proposed systematic grasping strategies for flexible materials by exploiting environmental constraints and analyzed their mechanical and kinematic models. To investigate the influence of materials and working conditions on grasping, an evaluation system for measuring grasping force and success rate was defined and experimentally evaluated. Finally, we summarized the specific workspaces and characteristics of different strategies that can satisfy various task requirements and lead to potential applications in household service robots for grasping planar flexible objects.

</details>


### [218] [DreamAvoid: Critical-Phase Test-Time Dreaming to Avoid Failures in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11750)
*Xianzhe Fan,Yuxiang Lu,Shenyuan Gao,Xiaoyang Wu,Ruihua Han,Manling Li,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamAvoid提出了一种测试时“梦境”框架，通过自主边界学习让VLA模型在关键阶段预测并避免失败，显著提升了细粒度操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在细粒度操作中容易出错，且仅用成功示范训练，缺乏对失败的显式感知，导致关键阶段的微小错误迅速演变为不可逆失败。

Method: (1) Dream Trigger判断是否进入关键阶段；(2) Action Proposer从VLA采样多个候选动作块；(3) Dream Evaluator在混合数据（成功、失败、边界案例）上联合训练，对候选动作进行短期未来“梦境”推演、评估并选择最优动作。

Result: 在真实操作任务和仿真基准上的广泛评估表明，DreamAvoid能有效避免失败，提升整体任务成功率。

Conclusion: 通过测试时梦境推演和边界学习，使VLA模型具备失败预见与规避能力，是提升细粒度操作鲁棒性的有效范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are often brittle in fine-grained manipulation, where minor action errors during the critical phases can rapidly escalate into irrecoverable failures. Since existing VLA models rely predominantly on successful demonstrations for training, they lack an explicit awareness of failure during these critical phases. To address this, we propose DreamAvoid, a critical-phase test-time dreaming framework that enables VLA models to anticipate and avoid failures. We also introduce an autonomous boundary learning paradigm to refine the system's understanding of the subtle boundary between success and failure. Specifically, we (1) utilize a Dream Trigger to determine whether the execution has entered a critical phase, (2) sample multiple candidate action chunks from the VLA via an Action Proposer, and (3) employ a Dream Evaluator, jointly trained on mixed data (success, failure, and boundary cases), to "dream" the short-horizon futures corresponding to the candidate actions, evaluate their values, and select the optimal action. We conduct extensive evaluations on real-world manipulation tasks and simulation benchmarks. The results demonstrate that DreamAvoid can effectively avoid failures, thereby improving the overall task success rate. Our code is available at https://github.com/XianzheFan/DreamAvoid.

</details>


### [219] [NavOL: Navigation Policy with Online Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11762)
*Xiaofei Wei,Chun Gu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavOL提出了一种在线模仿学习范式，通过与模拟器交互并收集在线专家演示来训练导航策略，无需奖励设计，缓解分布偏移，提高学习效率。在IsaacLab上利用并行渲染和域随机化实现了高效数据收集，并在仿真和真实实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线模仿学习存在分布偏移和 rollout 累积误差问题，强化学习需要奖励工程设计且学习效率低下，亟需一种无需奖励设计、能缓解分布偏移且学习高效的方法。

Method: NavOL采用在线模仿学习范式，基于预训练的导航扩散策略，在rollout更新循环中：rollout阶段策略在模拟器中执行并查询具有全局环境特权信息的全局规划器以获得最优路径段作为真值标签；update阶段策略在在线收集的观测-轨迹对上进行训练。

Result: 在8块RTX 4090 GPU上跨50个场景扩展，每小时收集超过2000条新轨迹（每条平均400+步）；在NavDP基准和提出的室内导航基准以及真实实验中均表现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: NavOL通过在线模仿学习有效解决了离线模仿学习的分布偏移问题和强化学习的奖励设计难题，在仿真和真实环境中均展现出持续的性能优势。

Abstract: Learning robust navigation policies remains a core challenge in robotics. Offline imitation learning suffers from distribution shift and compounding errors at rollout, while reinforcement learning requires reward engineering and learns inefficiently. In this paper, we propose NavOL, an online imitation learning paradigm that interacts with a simulator and updates itself using expert demonstrations gathered online. Built upon a pretrained navigation diffusion policy that maps local observations to future waypoints, NavOL trains in a rollout update loop: during rollout, the policy acts in the simulator and queries a global planner which has privileged access to the global environment for the optimal path segment as ground truth trajectory labels; during update, the policy is trained on the online collected observation trajectory pairs. This online imitation loop removes the need for reward design, improves learning efficiency, and mitigates distribution shift by training on the policy own explored rollouts. Built on IsaacLab with fast, high-fidelity parallel rendering and domain randomization of camera pose and start-goal pairs, our system scales across 50 scenes on 8 RTX 4090 GPUs, collecting over 2,000 new trajectories per hour, each averaging more than 400 steps. We also introduce an indoor visual navigation benchmark with predefined start and goal positions for zero-shot generalization. Extensive evaluations on simulation benchmarks, including the NavDP benchmark and our proposed benchmark, as well as carefully designed real-world experiments, demonstrate the effectiveness of NavOL, showing consistent performance gains in online imitation learning.

</details>


### [220] [See What Matters: Differentiable Grid Sample Pruning for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11817)
*Yixu Feng,Zinan Zhao,Yanxiang Ma,Chenghao Xia,Chengbin Du,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Differentiable Grid Sampler (GridS)，一种可插拔的视觉标记连续重采样模块，通过任务感知的坐标预测和可微插值，在保留关键几何信息的同时将视觉标记压缩至原始量的10%以下，实现76%的FLOPs降低且不损失成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型计算成本高，现有令牌剪枝方法在激进压缩时不可避免地丢弃接触点等关键几何细节，导致性能严重下降，从而限制了压缩率和加速潜力。

Method: 提出Differentiable Grid Sampler (GridS)，作为视觉编码器中的可插拔模块，通过自适应预测最小显著坐标集并利用可微插值提取特征，实现任务感知的连续视觉令牌重采样。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和真实机器人平台上，GridS以低于10%的原始视觉令牌数量，实现76%的FLOPs降低，且零成功率损失，验证了迄今报道的最低可行视觉令牌数量。

Conclusion: 通过将压缩重新构想为几何感知的连续令牌重采样，突破了激进压缩与性能退化之间的根本性权衡，实现了高效的VLA模型实时部署。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable promise in robotics manipulation, yet their high computational cost hinders real-time deployment. Existing token pruning methods suffer from a fundamental trade-off: aggressive compression using pruning inevitably discards critical geometric details like contact points, leading to severe performance degradation. This forces a compromise, limiting the achievable compression rate and thus the potential speedup. We argue that breaking this trade-off requires rethinking compression as a geometry-aware, continuous token resampling in the vision encoder. To this end, we propose the Differentiable Grid Sampler (GridS), a plug-and-play module that performs task-aware, continuous resampling of visual tokens in VLA. By adaptively predicting a minimal set of salient coordinates and extracting features via differentiable interpolation, GridS preserves essential spatial information while achieving drastic compression (with fewer than 10% original visual tokens). Experiments on both LIBERO benchmark and a real robotic platform demonstrate that validating the lowest feasible visual token count reported to date, GridS achieves a 76% reduction in FLOPs with no degradation in the success rate. The code is available at https://github.com/Fediory/Grid-Sampler.

</details>


### [221] [Mapping Embodied Affective Touch Strategies on a Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11825)
*Qiaoqiao Ren,Omar Eldardeer,Francesca Cocchella,Rea Francesco,Alessandra Sciutti,Tony Belpaeme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过32名参与者与全身触觉传感机器人iCub的交互实验，发现情感触摸表达强烈依赖于身体区域和物理约束：自由触摸时偏好上肢社交区域，不同身体区域（手臂vs躯干）分别通过运动特征和压力特征编码情感，且触摸策略无法直接迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRI研究通常只关注一两个孤立身体部位，限制了对情感触摸如何在整个类人身体上泛化的理解。

Method: 32名参与者与配备全身分布式触觉传感器的iCub机器人交互，在自由触摸、仅手臂触摸和仅躯干触摸三种条件下表达八种情绪。

Result: 身体区域和空间约束共同塑造触摸位置和动力学；自由触摸中偏好上肢区域；不同约束条件下触摸策略无法直接迁移；约30%参与者报告感知社会关系发生变化。

Conclusion: 情感触摸表达强烈依赖于身体区域，并受到具身约束的塑造。

Abstract: Affective touch in human-robot interaction is shaped not only by emotional intent, but also by robot embodiment, including touch location, physical constraints, and perceived agency or social role. Existing HRI studies typically focus on one or two isolated body parts, limiting understanding of how affective touch generalises across the full humanoid body. We present a study with 32 participants interacting with the iCub robot, which is equipped with full-body distributed tactile sensors. Participants expressed eight emotions under three conditions: free touch, arm-only touch, and torso-only touch. Results show that body region and spatial constraints jointly shaped both touch location and dynamics. In free touch, participants preferred socially accessible upper-body regions, while less frequently touched areas showed stronger emotion-specific selectivity. Emotion-related variation was more evident in motion features for arm-only touch and pressure features for torso-only touch. Touch strategies also did not transfer directly between free and constrained conditions, even within the same coarse body region. Participants reported increased closeness to the robot after interaction, with around 30 percent reporting a change in perceived social relationship. Together, these findings show that affective touch expression is strongly body-region dependent and shaped by embodiment constraints.

</details>


### [222] [Learning Action Manifold with Multi-view Latent Priors for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11832)
*Junjin Xiao,Dongyang Li,Yandan Yang,Shuang Zeng,Tong Lin,Xinyuan Chang,Feng Xiong,Mu Xu,Xing Wei,Zhiheng Ma,Qing Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对VLA模型在单目深度模糊和动作学习效率低下的问题，提出利用预训练多视图扩散模型合成潜在新视图，并设计几何引导门控Transformer（G3T）对齐多视图特征、过滤遮挡噪声，同时引入动作流形学习（AML）直接在有效动作流形上预测动作。实验在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实机器人任务上取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型面临两个核心挑战：1）单目输入的深度模糊性导致空间感知不准确；2）动作学习效率低，传统方法需要对噪声或速度等非结构化目标进行低效回归。

Method: 1）利用预训练多视图扩散模型合成潜在新视图，缓解单目深度模糊；2）提出几何引导门控Transformer（G3T），在3D几何引导下对齐多视图特征并自适应过滤遮挡噪声；3）提出动作流形学习（AML），直接在有效动作流形上预测动作，避免对非结构化目标的低效回归。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0以及真实机器人任务上，所提方法在成功率和鲁棒性上均超越当前SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 本文通过多视图几何引导和动作流形学习有效提升了VLA模型的空间感知和动作学习能力，实验验证了方法的优越性和实用性。

Abstract: This paper tackles spatial perception and manipulation challenges in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. To address depth ambiguity from monocular input, we leverage a pre-trained multi-view diffusion model to synthesize latent novel views and propose a Geometry-Guided Gated Transformer (G3T) that aligns multi-view features under 3D geometric guidance while adaptively filtering occlusion noise. To improve action learning efficiency, we introduce Action Manifold Learning (AML), which directly predicts actions on the valid action manifold, bypassing inefficient regression of unstructured targets like noise or velocity. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-robot tasks show our method achieves superior success rate and robustness over SOTA baselines. Project page: https://junjxiao.github.io/Multi-view-VLA.github.io/.

</details>


### [223] [From Reaction to Anticipation: Proactive Failure Recovery through Agentic Task Graph for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11951)
*Sheng Xu,Ruixing Jin,Huayi Zhou,Bo Yue,Guanren Qiao,Yunxin Tai,Yueci Deng,Kui Jia,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgentChord提出了一种基于预期性恢复分支的有向任务图模型，通过专门智能体的协作编排（作曲家、编曲者、指挥者），在操作任务执行前预先规划故障恢复策略，显著提升了长时域双臂操作任务的成功率和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作框架通常采用"检测-推理-恢复"的流水线模式，在动态非结构化环境中存在高延迟和鲁棒性不足的问题。受人类能够预期并主动规划潜在失败的能力启发，本文提出一种预期性故障处理机制。

Method: 将操作任务建模为有向任务图，在执行前通过预期性恢复分支（anticipatory recovery branches）增强该图，指定上下文感知的纠正行为。系统包含三类专门智能体：作曲家（构建名义任务图）、编曲者（用预期性恢复分支增强图）、指挥者（编译并协调可执行转换），利用低延迟监视器检测偏差并触发预编译恢复策略。

Result: 在多种长时域双臂操作任务上的实验表明，AgentChord显著提高了任务成功率和执行效率，验证了其在真实世界机器人系统中的可靠性和自主性提升。

Conclusion: AgentChord通过预期性规划而非反应式推理的方式处理操作故障，为提升机器人系统在动态环境中的鲁棒性和自主性提供了一种有效范式。

Abstract: Although robotic manipulation has made significant progress, reliable execution remains challenging because task failures are inevitable in dynamic and unstructured environments. To handle such failures, existing frameworks typically follow a stepwise detect-reason-recover pipeline, which often incurs high latency and limited robustness due to delayed reasoning and reactive planning. Inspired by the human capability to anticipate and proactively plan for potential failures, we introduce AgentChord, an agentic system that models a manipulation task as a directed task graph. Before execution, this graph is enriched with anticipatory recovery branches that specify context-aware corrective behaviors, enabling immediate and targeted responses when failures occur. Specifically, AgentChord operates through a choreography of specialized agents: a composer that structures the nominal task graph, an arranger that augments the graph with anticipatory recovery branches, and a conductor that compiles and coordinates executable transitions using low-latency monitors to detect deviations and trigger pre-compiled recoveries without re-planning. Empirical studies on diverse long-horizon bimanual manipulation tasks demonstrate that AgentChord substantially improves success rates and execution efficiency, advancing the reliability and autonomy of real-world robotic systems. The project page is available at: https://shengxu.net/AgentChord/.

</details>


### [224] [Cooperative Robotics Reinforced by Collective Perception for Traffic Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11972)
*Mohammad Khoshkdahan,John Pravin Arockiasamy,Andy Flores Comeca,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协作式人形机器人作为主动交通调节者，通过双摄像头基础设施感知和V2X通信，在非视距交叉口物理拦截试图盲区汇入的车辆，以避免碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLOS交叉口因视野受限存在重大安全隐患；V2X警告虽能降低风险但渗透率低且驾驶员可能忽视车内警报；集体感知（CP）能扩展联网车感知但无法影响非联网车辆。

Method: 系统包含双并行感知通路：1）双摄像头基础设施单元检测接近车辆的位置、速度和运动，发送CPM给机器人；2）机器人通过车载V2X接收CAM和DENM。融合模块整合数据建立主路实时视图，定义危险区（ZoD）预测碰撞风险，一旦检测到风险，机器人以类人STOP手势物理阻挡汇入路径直至危险解除。

Result: 系统在荷兰鹿特丹Future Mobility Park实际部署，实验表明视觉与V2X融合感知使机器人能够早期检测接近车辆、可靠预测危险并在真实NLOS条件下阻止不安全汇入。

Conclusion: 该概念填补了CP无法影响非联网车辆的空白，通过物理干预方式为NLOS交叉口安全提供补充方案。

Abstract: Collisions at non-line-of-sight (NLOS) intersections remain a major safety concern because drivers have limited visibility of approaching traffic. V2X based warnings can reduce these risks, yet many vehicles are not equipped with V2X and drivers may ignore in vehicle alerts. Collective perception (CP) can compensate for low V2X penetration by extending the awareness of connected vehicles, but it cannot influence unconnected vehicles. To fill this gap, our work introduces a complementary concept that adds a cooperative humanoid robot as an active traffic moderator capable of physically stopping a vehicle that attempts to merge into an unseen traffic stream. The system operates on two parallel perception pathways. A dual camera infrastructure unit detects the position, speed and motion of approaching vehicles and transmits this information to the robot as a collective perception message (CPM). The robot also receives cooperative awareness messages (CAM) from connected vehicles through its onboard V2X unit and can act as a relay for decentralized environmental notification messages (DENM) when safety events originate elsewhere along the road. A fusion module combines these streams to maintain a robust real time view of the main road. A Zone of Danger (ZoD) is defined and used to predict whether an approaching vehicle creates a collision risk for a merging road user. When such a risk is detected, the robot issues a human-like STOP gesture and blocks the merging path until the hazard disappears. The full system was deployed at the Future Mobility Park (FMP) in Rotterdam. Experiments show that the combined vision and V2X perception allows the robot to detect approaching vehicles early, predict hazards reliably and prevent unsafe merges in real world NLOS conditions.

</details>


### [225] [Closing the Motion Execution Gap: From Semantic Motion Task Constraints to Kinematic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12053)
*Simon Stelter,Vanessa Hassouna,Malte Huerkamp,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Giskard框架，通过Motion Statecharts弥合高层符号任务描述与可执行机器人运动之间的鸿沟，在8种机器人平台上验证了跨平台可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Motion Execution Gap，即高层符号任务描述（使用语义约束）与可执行机器人运动之间的脱节问题。

Method: 提出Motion Statecharts作为可执行的符号运动表示，支持任意排列运动约束、监视器或嵌套状态图（并行和顺序）；采用统一可微运动学世界模型实现世界中心运动规范和跨本体泛化；基于lMPC实现任务函数方法，利用加加速度界确保任务切换时的平滑过渡。

Result: 在8种不同机器人平台上成功部署，运行于多样化环境，验证了跨平台可迁移性。

Conclusion: Giskard框架有效弥合了符号任务描述与可执行运动之间的鸿沟，具备良好的跨平台迁移能力，已开源发布。

Abstract: This paper addresses the Motion Execution Gap, the disconnect between high-level symbolic task descriptions using semantic constraints and executable robot motions. Motion Statecharts are introduced as an executable symbolic representation for complex motions. They allow the arbitrary arrangement of motion constraints, monitors or nested statecharts in parallel and sequence. World-centric motion specification and generalization across embodiments are enabled through the use of a unified differentiable kinematic world model of both, robots and environments. Motion execution is realized through a lMPC-based implementation of the task-function approach, in which smooth transitions during task switches are ensured using jerk bounds. Cross-platform transferability was demonstrated by deploying the method on eight robot platforms, operating in diverse environments. The proposed framework is called Giskard and is available open source: https://github.com/cram2/cognitive_robot_abstract_machine.

</details>


### [226] [Control of Fully Actuated Aerial Vehicles: A Comparison of Model-based and Sensor-based Dynamic Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12071)
*Ali Sidar Yilmaz,Buday Turan,Lukas Pries,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在固定倾斜全驱动六旋翼平台上，实验比较了几何非线性动态逆（几何NDI）与增量非线性动态逆（INDI）两种控制策略。在相同外环结构和执行频率下，通过五种实验（标称姿态跟踪、参数失配、悬停抗扰、航点跟踪、控制频率降低及传感器退化）评估性能。结果表明：INDI在参数失配、阵风扰动和传感器退化下优势明显，且位置误差更低；但几何NDI在低控制频率下姿态跟踪更优。这是首个全姿态跟踪INDI在解耦平动/转动动力学上的实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全驱动多旋翼平台解耦了平动与转动控制，性能瓶颈从姿态响应转移到作动器动态和控制效能，亟需对比模型依赖型（几何NDI）与测量依赖型（INDI）两种控制策略在实际飞行中的鲁棒性与局限性，为工程部署提供指导。

Method: 在固定倾斜全驱动六旋翼上，保持相同外环结构和500 Hz执行频率，分别实现几何NDI和INDI控制器；设计五组实验：标称姿态阶跃跟踪、50%转子力系数失配下的姿态跟踪、外部侧向载荷悬停抗扰、阵风航点跟踪、控制频率扫描及传感器退化注入，定量对比位置和姿态误差。

Result: INDI在参数失配、阵风扰动和传感器退化下姿态误差更小，且在不同控制频率下位置误差均更低；几何NDI在控制频率降低时姿态跟踪精度优于INDI。

Conclusion: 测量驱动的INDI和模型驱动的几何NDI各有优劣：INDI鲁棒性更强，适合参数不确定和扰动大的场景；几何NDI在低控制频率下姿态精度更高。实际部署需根据传感器质量、模型精度和执行频率权衡选择。

Abstract: Fully actuated multirotor platforms decouple translational force generation from vehicle attitude, enabling independent control of position and orientation and shifting performance limitations from attitude authority to actuator dynamics and control effectiveness. This paper compares a model-based nonlinear dynamic inversion controller (geometric NDI) with a sensor-based incremental dynamic inversion controller (INDI) on a fixed-tilt fully actuated hexarotor. Both controllers share an identical outer-loop structure and are both executed at 500 Hz; therefore, performance differences can be attributed primarily to the inversion strategy. Controller performance is evaluated in five experiments covering attitude step tracking under nominal conditions and under a 50% mismatch in the rotor force coefficient, hover disturbance rejection under an external lateral load, waypoint tracking in the presence of wind gust disturbances, reduced control frequency, and injected sensor degradation. The results show that INDI offers clear advantages under parameter mismatch, gust disturbances, and sensor degradation, and maintains lower position errors across the controller-frequency sweep. However, its advantages are not universal: geometric NDI yields better attitude tracking at reduced control frequencies. To the authors' best knowledge, this work presents the first experimental validation of a full pose tracking INDI controller with decoupled translational and rotational dynamics. These findings highlight the trade-off between measurement-based and model-based inversion for robust control and rapid deployment of fully actuated UAVs.

</details>


### [227] [Learning What Matters: Adaptive Information-Theoretic Objectives for Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12084)
*Youwei Yu,Jionghao Wang,Zhengming Yu,Wenping Wang,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QOED，一种基于最优实验设计的自适应信息目标，通过Fisher信息矩阵的特征空间分析识别可观测子空间并选择可辨识参数方向，同时抑制非关键参数的干扰效应，在机器人的导航与操作任务中显著提升了探索效率和策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计可学习的信息论目标以引导机器人探索极具挑战性，因为高维机器人系统中许多参数方向弱可观测或不可辨识，且即使选择了可辨识方向，被忽略的方向仍会影响探索并扭曲信息度量。

Method: 提出QOED方法：(i) 对Fisher信息矩阵进行特征空间分析以识别可观测子空间并选择可辨识参数方向；(ii) 修改探索目标以强调这些方向同时抑制非关键参数的干扰效应。在有限干扰和临界/非临界方向耦合受限条件下，提供恒定因子近似。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的导航与操作任务中，可辨识方向选择和非关键参数抑制分别带来35.23%和21.98%的性能提升。作为模型策略优化的探索目标，QOED进一步提升了策略性能。

Conclusion: QOED通过聚焦可辨识参数方向并抑制干扰，有效解决了高维机器人系统中信息论探索目标的设计难题，在多个任务上取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Designing learnable information-theoretic objectives for robot exploration remains challenging. Such objectives aim to guide exploration toward data that reduces uncertainty in model parameters, yet it is often unclear what information the collected data can actually reveal. Although reinforcement learning (RL) can optimize a given objective, constructing objectives that reflect parametric learnability is difficult in high-dimensional robotic systems. Many parameter directions are weakly observable or unidentifiable, and even when identifiable directions are selected, omitted directions can still influence exploration and distort information measures. To address this challenge, we propose Quasi-Optimal Experimental Design (Q{\footnotesize OED}), an adaptive information objective grounded in optimal experimental design. Q{\footnotesize OED} (i) performs eigenspace analysis of the Fisher information matrix to identify an observable subspace and select identifiable parameter directions, and (ii) modifies the exploration objective to emphasize these directions while suppressing nuisance effects from non-critical parameters. Under bounded nuisance influence and limited coupling between critical and nuisance directions, Q{\footnotesize OED} provides a constant-factor approximation to the ideal information objective that explores all parameters. We evaluate Q{\footnotesize OED} on simulated and real-world navigation and manipulation tasks, where identifiable-direction selection and nuisance suppression yield performance improvements of \SI{35.23}{\percent} and \SI{21.98}{\percent}, respectively. When integrated as an exploration objective in model-based policy optimization, Q{\footnotesize OED} further improves policy performance over established RL baselines.

</details>


### [228] [World Action Models: The Next Frontier in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12090)
*Siyin Wang,Junhao Shi,Zhaoyang Fu,Xinzhe He,Feihong Liu,Chenchen Yang,Yikang Zhou,Zhaoye Fei,Jingjing Gong,Jinlan Fu,Mike Zheng Shou,Xuanjing Huang,Xipeng Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了“世界动作模型”（WAMs）这一新兴范式，它统一了状态预测与动作生成，通过对级联式和联合式WAMs的分类、数据生态分析和评估协议总结，为这一快速发展领域提供了首个系统性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型仅学习观测到动作的映射，缺乏对物理世界演变过程的显式建模。为弥补这一局限，研究者开始将世界模型（环境动态预测模型）集成到动作生成流程中，但相关文献碎片化，缺乏统一的概念框架。

Method: 本文首先正式定义WAMs并与相关概念进行区分；追溯VLA与世界模型研究的融合发展脉络；提出结构化分类法，将现有方法分为级联式和联合式WAMs，进一步按生成模态、条件机制和动作解码策略细分；系统分析数据生态（机器人遥操作、便携式人类演示、仿真、互联网级自我中心视频）；综合新兴评估协议（视觉保真度、物理常识、动作合理性）。

Result: 形成了WAMs领域首个系统性综述，梳理了关键架构范式及其权衡，明确了开放挑战和未来机遇。

Conclusion: WAMs作为统一预测状态建模与动作生成的具身基础模型，在智能体策略学习中具有重要前景。当前该领域仍面临架构碎片化、数据生态不平衡、评估标准不统一等挑战，未来需进一步发展统一的理论框架和评估基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved strong semantic generalization for embodied policy learning, yet they learn reactive observation-to-action mappings without explicitly modeling how the physical world evolves under intervention. A growing body of work addresses this limitation by integrating world models, predictive models of environment dynamics, into the action generation pipeline. We term this emerging paradigm World Action Models (WAMs): embodied foundation models that unify predictive state modeling with action generation, targeting a joint distribution over future states and actions rather than actions alone. However, the literature remains fragmented across architectures, learning objectives, and application scenarios, lacking a unified conceptual framework. We formally define WAMs and disambiguate them from related concepts, and trace the foundations and early integration of VLA and world model research that gave rise to this paradigm. We organize existing methods into a structured taxonomy of Cascaded and Joint WAMs, with further subdivision by generation modality, conditioning mechanism, and action decoding strategy. We systematically analyze the data ecosystem fueling WAMs development, spanning robot teleoperation, portable human demonstrations, simulation, and internet-scale egocentric video, and synthesize emerging evaluation protocols organized around visual fidelity, physical commonsense, and action plausibility. Overall, this survey provides the first systematic account of the WAMs landscape, clarifies key architectural paradigms and their trade-offs, and identifies open challenges and future opportunities for this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [229] [X-Imitator: Spatial-Aware Imitation Learning via Bidirectional Action-Pose Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12162)
*Kai Xiong,Hongjie Fang,Lixin Yang,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出X-Imitator双路径框架，将空间感知与动作生成建模为双向耦合循环，在24个模拟和3个真实任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将空间感知与动作执行视为解耦或单向过程，限制了机器人处理复杂操作任务的能力。

Method: 提出X-Imitator，一个双路径框架，通过当前位姿预测与过去动作的相互条件化，构建空间推理与动作生成之间的连续互逆优化双向循环，并设计为可无缝集成到各种视觉运动策略中的模块化架构。

Result: 在24个模拟任务和3个真实世界任务中，该框架显著优于原始策略和先前使用显式位姿引导的方法。

Conclusion: 将空间感知与动作执行建模为耦合双向循环能够有效提升机器人复杂操作任务的表现，模拟了人类内部前向模型。

Abstract: Effectively handling the interplay between spatial perception and action generation remains a critical bottleneck in robotic manipulation. Existing methods typically treat spatial perception and action execution as decoupled or strictly unidirectional processes, fundamentally restricting a robot's ability to master complex manipulation tasks. To address this, we propose X-Imitator, a versatile dual-path framework that models spatial perception and action execution as a tightly coupled bidirectional loop. By reciprocally conditioning current pose predictions on past actions and vice versa, this framework enables continuous mutual refinement between spatial reasoning and action generation. This joint modeling exactly mimics human internal forward models. Designed as a modular architecture, the system can be seamlessly integrated into various visuomotor policies. Extensive experiments across 24 simulated and 3 real-world tasks demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms both vanilla policies and prior methods utilizing explicit pose guidance. The code will be open sourced.

</details>


### [230] [From Imagined Futures to Executable Actions: Mixture of Latent Actions for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12167)
*Yajie Li,Bozhou Zhang,Chun Gu,Zipei Ma,Jiahui Zhang,Jiankang Deng,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MoLA（Mixture of Latent Actions），一种面向控制的人机交互接口，通过混合预训练逆动力学模型从生成的未来视频中推断隐式动作表示，弥合视频想象与策略执行之间的鸿沟，在模拟和真实机器人操控任务上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型用于机器人操控时存在视觉真实性与控制相关性之间的不匹配问题：直接以预测帧为条件或直接解码为动作，都因强调感知保真度而非以动作为中心的因果状态转换，导致控制间接且不稳定。

Method: MoLA利用一组预训练的、多模态（语义、深度、光流）的逆动力学模型构成混合架构，从生成的未来视频帧序列中推断出隐式动作表示，作为结构化且物理可解释的动作表征，连接视频想象与策略执行。

Result: 在LIBERO、CALVIN、LIBERO-Plus等模拟基准以及真实机器人操控任务上，MoLA在任务成功率、时间一致性和泛化能力方面均取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: MoLA通过控制导向的隐动作接口有效解决了视频生成模型在机器人操控中的视觉-控制不匹配问题，为利用视频想象进行动作执行提供了可靠且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Video generation models offer a promising imagination mechanism for robot manipulation by predicting long-horizon future observations, but effectively exploiting these imagined futures for action execution remains challenging. Existing approaches either condition policies on predicted frames or directly decode generated videos into actions, both suffering from a mismatch between visual realism and control relevance. As a result, predicted observations emphasize perceptual fidelity rather than action-centric causes of state transitions, leading to indirect and unstable control. To address this gap, we propose MoLA (Mixture of Latent Actions), a control-oriented interface that transforms imagined future videos into executable representations. Instead of passing predicted frames directly to the policy, MoLA leverages a mixture of pretrained inverse dynamics models to infer a mixture of latent actions implied by generated visual transitions. These modality-aware inverse dynamics models capture complementary semantic, depth, and flow cues, providing a structured and physically grounded action representation that bridges video imagination and policy execution. We evaluate our approach on simulated benchmarks (LIBERO, CALVIN, and LIBERO-Plus) and real-world robot manipulation tasks, achieving consistent gains in task success, temporal consistency, and generalization.

</details>


### [231] [DexTwist: Dexterous Hand Retargeting for Twist Motion via Mixed Reality-based Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12182)
*Dongmyoung Lee,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对混合现实灵巧遥操作中传统重定向方法在接触式旋转操作（如开瓶、拧钥匙、拧螺丝）中因具身差距导致指尖滑动和抓取不稳定的问题，提出DexTwist功能扭转重定向框架，通过检测三脚捏合、估计操作者意图的螺旋轴和扭转量，并应用实时残差关节空间优化，在仿真和实物实验中显著改善了转角跟踪和螺旋轴稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于运动学相似性最小化（关节角或指尖位置误差）的重定向方法在接触式旋转操作中失效，原因是具身差距（连杆长度、关节轴/限位、指尖几何不匹配）导致直接姿态模仿产生切向指尖滑动而非稳定的物体旋转，进而引发螺旋轴漂移、接触滑移和抓取不稳定。

Method: 提出DexTwist框架：检测三脚捏合手势，估计操作者意图的螺旋轴和扭转量；设计实时残差关节空间优化，跟踪旋转进度同时正则化机器人三脚几何结构；优化目标包含转角、螺旋轴一致性、指尖闭合度和三脚稳定性四个项。

Result: 在仿真和实物实验中，与基于向量的重定向基线相比，DexTwist在转角跟踪精度和螺旋轴稳定性方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模旋转操作的功能性目标（螺旋轴一致性、抓取稳定性）而非仅优化运动学相似性，有效弥合了具身差距，为MR灵巧遥操作中的接触式旋转操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dexterous teleoperation via Mixed Reality (MR)-based interfaces offers a scalable paradigm for transferring human manipulation skills to dexterous robot hands. However, conventional retargeting approaches that minimize kinematic dissimilarity (e.g., joint angle or fingertip position error) often fail in contact-rich rotational manipulation, such as cap opening, key turning, and bolt screwing. This failure stems from the embodiment gap: mismatched link lengths, joint axes/limits, and fingertip geometry can cause direct pose imitation to induce tangential fingertip sliding rather than stable object rotation, resulting in screw axis drift, contact slip, and grasp instability. To address this, we propose DexTwist, a functional twist-retargeting framework for MR-based dexterous teleoperation. DexTwist detects a tripod pinch, estimates the operator's intended screw axis and twist magnitude, and applies a real-time residual joint-space refinement that tracks turning progress while regularizing the robot tripod geometry. The refinement minimizes a virtual-object objective defined by turning angle, screw axis consistency, fingertip closure, and tripod stability. Simulation and real-world experiments show that DexTwist improves turning angle tracking and screw axis stability compared with a vector-based retargeting baseline.

</details>


### [232] [TMRL: Diffusion Timestep-Modulated Pretraining Enables Exploration for Efficient Policy Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12236)
*Matthew M. Hong,Jesse Zhang,Anusha Nagabandi,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CSP预训练方法（向策略输入注入前向扩散噪声）和TMRL微调方法（通过调节扩散时间步控制探索），实现机器人策略从BC预训练到RL微调的高效衔接，在实际复杂操作任务中1小时内完成微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BC预训练产生的动作分布狭窄，缺乏覆盖范围，导致后续RL微调时探索不足，继承预训练瓶颈。

Method: 1) CSP预训练：向策略输入注入前向扩散噪声，在精确模仿与广泛动作覆盖之间建立连续谱；2) TMRL微调：训练智能体动态调节扩散时间步，在RL微调期间显式控制探索程度。

Result: TMRL提升了RL微调的样本效率，在不到1小时内完成实际复杂操作任务的微调，且可无缝集成状态、3D点云、图像VLA策略等多种策略输入。

Conclusion: 通过扩散噪声注入与时间步调制，统一了BC预训练与RL微调，解决了预训练策略探索不足的问题，实现了高效的机器人策略微调。

Abstract: Fine-tuning pre-trained robot policies with reinforcement learning (RL) often inherits the bottlenecks introduced by pre-training with behavioral cloning (BC), which produces narrow action distributions that lack the coverage necessary for downstream exploration. We present a unified framework that enables the exploration necessary to enable efficient robot policy finetuning by bridging BC pre-training and RL fine-tuning. Our pre-training method, Context-Smoothed Pre-training (CSP), injects forward-diffusion noise into policy inputs, creating a continuum between precise imitation and broad action coverage. We then fine-tune pre-trained policies via Timestep-Modulated Reinforcement Learning (TMRL), which trains the agent to dynamically adjust this conditioning during fine-tuning by modulating the diffusion timestep, granting explicit control over exploration. Integrating seamlessly with arbitrary policy inputs, e.g., states, 3D point clouds, or image-based VLA policies, we show that TMRL improves RL fine-tuning sample efficiency. Notably, TMRL enables successful real-world fine-tuning on complex manipulation tasks in under one hour. Videos and code available at https://weirdlabuw.github.io/tmrl/.

</details>


### [233] [SI-Diff: A Framework for Learning Search and High-Precision Insertion with a Force-Domain Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12247)
*Yibo Liu,Stanko Oparnica,Simon Shewchun-Jakaitis,Guoyi Fu,Jie Wang,Jun Yang,Anand Jagannathan,Tony Hong-Yau Lo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SI-Diff框架，通过力域扩散策略在同一模型中同时学习搜索和高精度插入两种装配任务，利用模式条件机制和教师策略生成的多样化轨迹，将x-y偏移容忍度从2mm提升至5mm，并展现对未见形状的零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的装配任务中，搜索和高精度插入涉及不同的动作模式，现有方法通常分别处理这两个任务，需要在模型或权重间切换。作者希望在同一框架下同时支持两种任务，以实现更智能的装配系统。

Method: 提出SI-Diff框架，采用力域扩散策略；引入模式条件机制（mode-conditioning）使单一策略能够捕获不同的动作行为；开发新的搜索教师策略生成多样化轨迹；通过教师策略提供的成功且高效的演示进行训练，学习从触觉和末端速度观测到有效动作行为的映射。

Result: 与最先进的基线TacDiffusion相比，将x-y偏移容忍度从2mm扩展到5mm；在未见形状上展现出强大的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: SI-Diff能够在单一框架下同时处理搜索和高精度插入任务，显著提升了装配任务的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为智能装配系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich assembly is fundamental in robotics but poses significant challenges due to uncertainties in relative poses, such as misalignments and small clearances in peg-in-hole tasks. Existing approaches typically address search and high-precision insertion separately, because these tasks involve distinct action patterns. However, supporting both tasks within a single model, without switching models or weights, is desirable for intelligent assembly systems. In this work, we propose SI-Diff, a framework that learns both search and high-precision insertion through a force-domain diffusion policy. To this end, we introduce a new mode-conditioning mechanism that enables the policy to capture distinct action behaviors under a single framework. Moreover, we develop a new search teacher policy that can generate diverse trajectories. By training on successful and efficient demonstrations provided by the teacher policy, the model learns the mapping from tactile and end-effector velocity observations to effective action behaviors. We conduct thorough experiments to show that SI-Diff extends the tolerance to x-y misalignments from 2 mm to 5 mm compared to the state-of-the-art baseline, TacDiffusion, while also demonstrating strong zero-shot transferability to unseen shapes.

</details>


### [234] [Real-Time Whole-Body Teleoperation of a Humanoid Robot Using IMU-Based Motion Capture with Sim2Sim and Sim2Real Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12347)
*Hamza Ahmed Durrani,Suleman Khan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于IMU全身动捕服的实时全身遥操作系统，通过运动处理、运动学重定向和控制管线，将人体运动直接映射到Unitree G1人形机器人上，在仿真和实物平台上均实现了稳定、低延迟的全身动作复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身遥操作面临运动学不匹配、惯性传感器噪声累积、控制延迟和仿真到现实迁移等挑战，现有方法难以实现稳定、低延迟的全身遥操作。

Method: 采用Virdyn IMU全身动捕服采集人体运动数据，设计自定义运动处理、运动学重定向和控制管线，实现无离线缓冲、无学习组件的连续低延迟遥操作。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真环境中验证系统性能（sim2sim），随后不经修改直接部署到实物平台（sim2real），成功实现了行走、站立、坐姿、转身、鞠躬及协调的全身表达性动作等多样化运动。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了利用商用可穿戴动捕硬件实现人形机器人全身遥操作的实用可扩展框架。

Abstract: Stable, low-latency whole-body teleoperation of humanoid robots is an open research challenge, complicated by kinematic mismatches between human and robot morphologies, accumulated inertial sensor noise, non-trivial control latency, and persistent sim-to-real transfer gaps. This paper presents a complete real-time whole-body teleoperation system that maps human motion, recorded with a Virdyn IMU-based full-body motion capture suit, directly onto a Unitree G1 humanoid robot. We introduce a custom motion-processing, kinematic retargeting, and control pipeline engineered for continuous, low-latency operation without any offline buffering or learning-based components. The system is first validated in simulation using the MuJoCo physics model of the Unitree G1 (sim2sim), and then deployed without modification on the physical platform (sim2real). Experimental results demonstrate stable, synchronized reproduction of a broad motion repertoire, including walking, standing, sitting, turning, bowing, and coordinated expressive full-body gestures. This work establishes a practical, scalable framework for whole-body humanoid teleoperation using commodity wearable motion capture hardware.

</details>


### [235] [GuidedVLA: Specifying Task-Relevant Factors via Plug-and-Play Action Attention Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12369)
*Xiaosong Jia,Bowen Yang,Zuhao Ge,Xian Nie,Yuchen Zhou,Cunxin Fan,Yufeng Li,Yilin Chai,Chao Jing,Zijian Liang,Qingwen Bu,Haidong Cao,Chao Wu,Qifeng Li,Zhenjie Yang,Chenhe Zhang,Hongyang Li,Zuxuan Wu,Junchi Yan,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GuidedVLA通过为动作解码器中的注意力头设计专门的辅助监督信号（物体定位、空间几何、时间技能逻辑），显式引导模型关注任务相关因素，从而提升VLA模型的泛化能力，在仿真和真实机器人实验中均优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型依赖端到端监督隐式学习动作解码，容易过拟合虚假相关（如视觉捷径或环境噪声），导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出GuidedVLA框架，将动作解码器拆解为功能组件，为各个注意力头分别设计人工定义的辅助信号进行监督。具体实例化三个专用头：物体定位头、空间几何头和时间技能逻辑头。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，GuidedVLA在领域内和领域外场景下的成功率均超过强VLA基线；专用因素的质量与任务性能正相关；该机制能产生解耦的高质量特征。

Conclusion: 显式引导动作解码器学习是构建更鲁棒和更通用的VLA模型的有前景方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models aim for general robot learning by aligning action as a modality within powerful Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Existing VLAs rely on end-to-end supervision to implicitly enable the action decoding process to learn task-relevant features. However, without explicit guidance, these models often overfit to spurious correlations, such as visual shortcuts or environmental noise, limiting their generalization. In this paper, we introduce GuidedVLA, a framework designed to manually guide the action generation to focus on task-relevant factors. Our core insight is to treat the action decoder not as a monolithic learner, but as an assembly of functional components. Individual attention heads are supervised by manually defined auxiliary signals to capture distinct factors. As an initial study, we instantiate this paradigm with three specialized heads: object grounding, spatial geometry, and temporal skill logic. Across simulation and real-robot experiments, GuidedVLA improves success rates in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings compared to strong VLA baselines. Finally, we show that the quality of these specialized factors correlates positively with task performance and that our mechanism yields decoupled, high-quality features. Our results suggest that explicitly guiding action-decoder learning is a promising direction for building more robust and general VLA models.

</details>


### [236] [SafeManip: A Property-Driven Benchmark for Temporal Safety Evaluation in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12386)
*Chengyue Huang,Khang Vo Huynh,Sebastian Elbaum,Zsolt Kira,Lu Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeManip提出了一种基于属性的时序安全基准测试框架，使用线性时序逻辑（LTLf）对机器人操作中的八类安全属性进行形式化建模与监控评估。实验表明，即使任务成功率高的策略，其成功执行中仍存在大量不安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作评估主要关注任务成功与否，忽略了执行过程中的时序安全问题（如污染接触、抓取释放时机不当等），缺乏对操作安全性的形式化、可复用的评估手段。

Method: 定义基于LTLf的时序安全模板，将机器人轨迹映射为符号谓词序列，通过LTLf监控器对八类安全属性（碰撞接触、抓取稳定、释放稳定、交叉污染、动作起始、机构恢复、物体包含、封闭空间进入）进行验证。模板支持任务特定实例化，可跨任务和环境泛化。

Result: 在6种视觉-语言-动作策略（π₀, π₀.₅, GR00T等）和50个RoboCasa365家庭任务上评估，发现即使强模型也常出现不安全行为。任务成功率的提升并不可靠地转化为安全执行，许多成功轨迹仍不安全，长程和复杂任务暴露更多违规。

Conclusion: SafeManip提供了一种可复用的评估层，用于诊断时序安全故障并衡量超越任务完成的安全成功，弥补了现有评估体系对操作过程安全性关注的不足。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is typically evaluated by task success, but successful completion does not guarantee safe execution. Many safety failures are temporal: a robot may touch a clean surface after contamination or release an object before it is fully inside an enclosure. We introduce SafeManip, a property-driven benchmark to explicitly evaluate temporal safety properties in robotic manipulation, moving beyond prior evaluations that largely focus on task completion or per-state constraint violations. SafeManip defines reusable safety templates over finite executions using Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces (LTLf). It maps observed rollouts to symbolic predicate traces and evaluates them with LTLf-based monitors. Its property suite covers eight manipulation safety categories: collision and contact safety, grasp stability, release stability, cross-contamination, action onset, mechanism recovery, object containment, and enclosure access. Templates can be instantiated with task-specific objects, fixtures, regions, or skills, allowing the same safety specifications to generalize across tasks and environments. We evaluate SafeManip on six vision-language-action policies, including $π_0$, $π_{0.5}$, GR00T, and their training variants, across 50 RoboCasa365 household tasks. Results show that even strong models often behave unsafely. Task-success gains do not reliably translate into safer execution: many successful rollouts remain unsafe, while longer-horizon or more complex tasks expose more violations. SafeManip provides a reusable evaluation layer for diagnosing temporal safety failures and measuring safe success beyond task completion.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [A Cascaded Generative Approach for e-Commerce Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11118)
*Moein Hasani,Hamidreza Shahidi,Trace Levinson,Yuan Zhong,Guanghua Shu,Vinesh Gudla,Tejaswi Tenneti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种级联式商品陈列框架，将电商店铺首页构建分解为两个生成任务：版位级主题生成和版位约束关键词生成，结合教师-学生微调提升可扩展性，并与传统排序模型融合，在线实验显示购物车添加量提升+2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商店铺首页由多个独立组件（静态主题、检索系统、逐点排序器）拼装而成，这种范式虽然能优化聚合偏好，但灵活性差，限制了页面个性化与语义连贯性，难以适应动态目标和不断变化的商品陈列需求。

Method: 将店铺首页构建分解为两个生成任务：(i) 版位级主题生成；(ii) 每个版位的约束关键词生成以驱动产品检索。采用教师-学生微调提升可扩展性，满足生产环境延迟和成本约束；同时提出AI驱动的内容评估与质量过滤框架，并将生成式输出与传统排序模型融合以保持混合基础设施。

Result: 微调模型消融实验性能接近闭源大语言模型；在线实验中，该框架相比强基线在每页面浏览量的购物车添加量上获得约+2.7%的提升。

Conclusion: 该级联商品陈列框架有效提升了电商店铺首页的个性化与语义连贯性，同时通过教师-学生微调和混合架构保证了生产环境下的可扩展性与安全性，为实现动态内容的自动化安全部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Personalized storefronts in large e-commerce marketplaces are often assembled from many independent components: static themes per page section ("placement"), retrieval systems to fetch eligible products per placement, and pointwise rankers to order content. While effective in optimizing for aggregate preferences, this paradigm is rigid and can limit personalization and semantic cohesion across the page. This makes it poorly suited to support dynamic objectives and merchandising requirements over time. To address this, we introduce a cascaded merchandising framework that decomposes storefront construction into two generative tasks: (i) placement-level theme generation and (ii) constrained keyword generation per placement to power product retrieval. Teacher-student fine-tuning is leveraged to improve scalability of this framework under production latency and cost constraints. Fine-tuned model ablations are shown to approach closed-weight LLM performance. We further contribute frameworks for AI-driven content evaluation and quality filtering, enabling safe and automated deployment of dynamic content at scale. Generative output is fused with traditional ranking models to preserve hybrid infrastructure. In online experiments, this framework yields an estimated +2.7% lift in cart adds per page view over a strong baseline.

</details>


### [238] [EVOCHAMBER: Test-Time Co-evolution of Multi-Agent System at Individual, Team, and Population Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11136)
*Yaolun Zhang,Tianyi Xu,Shengyu Dai,Zhenwen Shao,Qingyun Wu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EVOCHAMBER，一种无需训练的多智能体测试时进化框架，包含CODREAM协议实现非对称跨智能体知识迁移，在数学、代码和多领域推理任务上显著超越基线，并展现出从同质智能体自发涌现专业化分工的 emergent 现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时方法要么将经验限制在单个智能体内（丧失跨智能体学习），要么对称广播给所有智能体（抹除专业化分工）。多智能体系统的进化涉及谁协作、如何协作、知识如何流动等单智能体无法对应的组件，需要一种能保留专业化同时填补知识差距的框架。

Method: EVOCHAMBER包含三层进化：1) CODREAM协议——在团队失败或分歧时触发，智能体协作反思并将洞见从强智能体非对称路由到弱智能体；2) 团队级算子——基于特定场景组装团队并在线选择协作结构；3) 种群级生命周期算子——在性能压力下进行fork、merge、prune和seed操作。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，EVOCHAMBER在竞赛数学任务达63.9%，代码任务达75.7%，多领域推理达87.1%。数学任务相对最优基线提升32%。消融实验确认非对称跨智能体迁移是主要驱动力。从相同初始化的智能体出发，自发涌现4-5个稳定的专业分化智能体。

Conclusion: 多智能体测试时进化不是单智能体进化的简单复制，而是能够产生如专业化分工等 emergent 现象。EVOCHAMBER通过非对称知识迁移和三层进化机制，有效实现了跨智能体学习与专业化保留的平衡，验证了多智能体进化的独特价值。

Abstract: We argue that multi-agent test-time evolution is not single-agent evolution replicated N times. A single-agent learner can only evolve its own context and memory. A multi-agent system additionally evolves who collaborates, how they collaborate, and how knowledge flows across the population. These components have no single-agent counterpart and can produce phenomena such as emergent specialization. Yet prior test-time methods either confine experiences to individual agents, forfeiting cross-agent learning, or broadcast symmetrically to all agents, erasing the specialization that makes collaboration valuable. We present EVOCHAMBER, a training-free framework that instantiates test-time evolution at three levels over a coevolving agent pool. At its core is CODREAM (Collaborative Dreaming), a post-task protocol triggered on team failure or disagreement, in which agents collaboratively reflect, distill insights, and route them asymmetrically from strong to weak agents on the failed niche, preserving specialization while filling knowledge gaps. Team-level operators assemble niche-conditioned teams and select collaboration structures online. Population-level lifecycle operators fork, merge, prune, and seed agents under performance pressure. On three heterogeneous task streams with Qwen3-8B, EVOCHAMBER reaches 63.9% on competition math, 75.7% on code, and 87.1% on multi-domain reasoning, outperforming the best baseline by 32% relative on math and confirming asymmetric cross-agent transfer as the primary driver in ablation. Starting from several identically initialized agents, four to five stable niche specialists spontaneously emerge, a structural signature of multi-agent evolution that no single-agent learner can express. See our code at: https://github.com/Mercury7353/EvoChamber

</details>


### [239] [RankQ: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning via Self-Supervised Action Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11151)
*Andrew Choi,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RankQ，一种通过自监督多术语排序损失增强时序差分学习的离线到在线Q学习方法。通过学习结构化动作偏好而非均匀惩罚OOD动作，RankQ在稀疏奖励D4RL基准上达到或超越七种方法的性能，并在视觉机器人学习中显著提升预训练VLA模型的微调效果（仿真成功率最高提升42.7%，真实世界堆叠成功率从43.1%提升至84.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线到在线强化学习中，在有限数据集覆盖下学习精确的critic面临挑战。现有方法通过悲观主义下加权OOD动作来缓解价值高估问题，但这本质上起到了行为克隆锚定的作用，当数据集动作次优时会阻碍下游在线策略改进。

Method: 提出RankQ，在时序差分学习中引入自监督多术语排序损失，学习结构化动作排序（相对动作偏好），而非均匀惩罚未见过的动作，从而使Q函数的动作梯度指向更高质量的行为。

Result: 在稀疏奖励D4RL基准上表现优于或持平七种方法；在视觉机器人学习中，低数据 regime 下仿真成功率平均比次优方法高42.7%，高数据 regime 下性能提升13.7%，真实世界堆叠成功率从43.1%提升至84.7%。

Conclusion: RankQ通过学习相对动作偏好有效缓解了离线到在线强化学习中悲观主义方法的行为克隆锚定问题，显著提升了样本效率和在线策略改进能力，在仿真和真实机器人任务中均取得优异表现。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) improves sample efficiency by leveraging pre-collected datasets prior to online interaction. A key challenge, however, is learning an accurate critic in large state--action spaces with limited dataset coverage. To mitigate harmful updates from value overestimation, prior methods impose pessimism by down-weighting out-of-distribution (OOD) actions relative to dataset actions. While effective, this essentially acts as a behavior cloning anchor and can hinder downstream online policy improvement when dataset actions are suboptimal. We propose RankQ, an offline-to-online Q-learning objective that augments temporal-difference learning with a self-supervised multi-term ranking loss to enforce structured action ordering. By learning relative action preferences rather than uniformly penalizing unseen actions, RankQ shapes the Q-function such that action gradients are directed toward higher-quality behaviors. Across sparse reward D4RL benchmarks, RankQ achieves performance competitive with or superior to seven prior methods. In vision-based robot learning, RankQ enables effective offline-to-online fine-tuning of a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model in a low-data regime, achieving on average a 42.7% higher simulation success rate than the next best method. In a high-data setting, RankQ improves simulation performance by 13.7% over the next best method and achieves strong sim-to-real transfer, increasing real-world cube stacking success from 43.1% to 84.7% relative to the VLA's initial performance.

</details>


### [240] [OLIVIA: Online Learning via Inference-time Action Adaptation for Decision Making in LLM ReAct Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11169)
*Sheldon Yu,Junda Wu,Xintong Li,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Sizhe Zhou,Tong Yu,Jiawei Han,Jingbo Shang,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OLIVIA将ReAct风格LLM智能体的最终动作选择层建模为上下文线性Bandit，在部署阶段通过在线学习和不确定性感知探索，以轻量级方式提升多步任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体部署时的小动作选择错误会累积导致工具浪费、延迟和可靠性下降，但现有推理时适应方法（提示/检索）缺乏显式决策层来评分候选动作、表示不确定性或从动作级反馈在线更新。

Method: 将LLM最终动作选择层建模为上下文线性Bandit，以冻结的隐藏状态作为决策上下文，使用上置信界（UCB）探索策略，支持从动作级反馈进行轻量级在线更新。

Result: 在四个基准测试上，OLIVIA一致优于静态ReAct和基于提示的推理时基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式在线决策层为LLM智能体部署时提供了一种有效的替代方案，优于纯提示或检索式适应方法。

Abstract: Large language model agents interleave reasoning, action selection, and observation to solve sequential decision-making tasks. In deployed settings where agents repeatedly handle related multi-step tasks, small action-selection errors can accumulate into wasted tool calls, latency, and reduced reliability. Despite this need for deployment-time improvement, existing inference-time adaptation methods for LLM agents mainly rely on prompting or retrieval, which influence behavior indirectly through context manipulation. For ReAct-style agents, such approaches do not expose an explicit decision layer that can score candidate actions, represent uncertainty, or be updated online from action-level feedback. As a result, they provide limited support for trackable, fine-grained, and uncertainty-aware adaptation during deployment. We propose OLIVIA, an inference-time action adaptation framework for ReAct-style agents. OLIVIA models the LLM's final action-selection layer as a contextual linear bandit over candidate actions, with frozen hidden states as decision contexts. This choice is particularly suitable for deployment because it adapts behavior directly at the action-selection interface, preserves the underlying reasoning process, and provides explicit uncertainty estimates and lightweight online updates from action-level feedback. With upper-confidence-bound exploration, OLIVIA improves the policy sample-efficiently with minimal computational overhead. We instantiate OLIVIA on four benchmarks and show that it consistently improves task performance over static ReAct and prompt-based inference-time baselines. Our results suggest that explicit online decision layers provide an effective alternative to purely prompt- or retrieval-based adaptation for LLM agents during deployment.

</details>


### [241] [The Many Faces of On-Policy Distillation: Pitfalls, Mechanisms, and Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11182)
*Siqi Zhu,Xuyan Ye,Hongyu Lu,Weiye Shi,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了在线策略蒸馏(OPD)和在线策略自蒸馏(OPSD)在大型语言模型后训练中的有效性与失败机制，发现OPD对教师选择和损失函数高度敏感，OPSD在实例特定特权信息缺失时失败，并识别了三种核心失败机制及缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中OPD和OPSD的效果结论不一，既有成功案例（如系统提示和知识内化），也有不稳定和性能退化现象。作者旨在系统性地厘清这些方法何时有效、何时失效及其根本原因。

Method: 通过全面的实证研究，分析OPD和OPSD在不同任务（数学推理、系统提示、对齐偏好）下的表现，识别失败机制（分布不匹配、优化不稳定、特权信息聚合不足），并提出缓解策略（stop-gradient TopK目标、RLVR适配教师、SFT稳定学生）。

Result: OPD在数学推理中对教师选择和损失函数高度敏感；OPSD在缺乏实例特定特权信息时失败；但在共享潜在规则（如系统提示或对齐偏好）下有效。三种失败机制被识别并得到实验验证。

Conclusion: OPD和OPSD的效果高度依赖任务特性与设置，特定缓解策略可改善其稳定性与性能，但需谨慎选择应用场景。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) and on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) have emerged as promising post-training methods for large language models, offering dense token-level supervision on trajectories sampled from the model's own policy. However, existing results on their effectiveness remain mixed: while OP(S)D has shown promise in system prompt and knowledge internalization, recent studies also report instability and degradation. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical study of when OPD and OPSD work, when they fail, and why. We find that OPD on mathematical reasoning is highly sensitive to teacher choice and loss formulation, whereas OPSD fails in our tested settings due to test-time absence of instance-specific privileged information (PI). In contrast, OPSD is effective when PI represents a shared latent rule, such as a system prompt or alignment preference. We identify three failure mechanisms: (1) distribution mismatch between teacher and student caused by conditioning on student-generated prefixes, (2) optimization instability from biased TopK reverse-KL gradients, and (3) an OPSD-specific limitation where the student learns a PI-free policy that aggregates PI-conditioned teachers, which is insufficient when PI is instance-specific. We further show that stop-gradient TopK objectives, RLVR-adapted teachers, and SFT-stabilized students mitigate these failures.

</details>


### [242] [Don't Look at the Numbers: Visual Anchoring Bias and Layer-wise Representation in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11218)
*M. Shalankin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 嵌入在图像上的数字锚点会系统性偏差六个视觉语言模型的图像质量判断，效应量是严重图像质量退化的2.5倍。层分析显示锚点分类饱和层与质量预测最优层分离，融合分析揭示了架构依赖的整合模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉语言模型中图像上的数字锚点如何影响模型的质量判断，并分析这种锚定偏差的因果机制和表征动态。

Method: 使用六个视觉语言模型进行实验，采用ANOVA分析锚点效应，通过层探针分析不同层次的表征，进行融合分析研究锚点信息的整合模式。

Result: 锚点效应显著（eta^2=0.18-0.77），效应量是严重图像质量退化的2.5倍；锚点分类饱和层（L12-L34）对质量预测不优，最优层更深（R^2=0.69-0.91）；融合模式存在架构依赖差异。

Conclusion: 建立了视觉锚定偏差的因果解释，将行为易感性与表征动态联系起来，揭示了不同VLM架构中锚点信息整合的差异性。

Abstract: Embedded numeric anchors on images systematically bias Vision-Language Model quality judgments across six VLMs from five architectural families (ANOVA eta^2 = 0.18-0.77, all p < 0.001). Anchor effects are 2.5x larger than severe image quality degradation, confirming bias is not reducible to visual changes. Layer-wise probing reveals consistent dissociation: layers where anchor classification saturates (L12-L34) are suboptimal for quality prediction, with optimal layers deeper (R^2 = 0.69-0.91). Fusion analysis identifies architecture-dependent integration -- instant fusion at L1-L2 in two models versus partial or no fusion in three others. These results establish a causal account of visual anchoring bias, linking behavioral susceptibility to representation dynamics.

</details>


### [243] [Do Vision-Language-Models show human-like logical problem-solving capability in point and click puzzle games?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11223)
*Dominik Helfenstein,Marco Menner,Maximilian Triebel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VLATIM基准，用于评估VLM在物理解谜游戏《The Incredible Machine 2》中的类人逻辑推理能力，结果发现大模型在高级规划与精确视觉定位之间存在显著差距，尚未达到人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准往往忽视了点按式解谜游戏中所需的复杂物理推理能力，存在高级逻辑推理与连续动作空间精确鼠标交互之间的关键空白。

Method: 基于经典物理解谜游戏TIM2构建VLATIM基准，包含五个渐进式部分，评估从基础视觉定位、领域理解到多步操作和完整解谜的能力。

Result: 大型闭源模型在规划能力上表现优越，但在精确视觉定位上存在困难，推理与执行之间存在显著差距，尚未展现类人级问题解决能力。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在物理解谜任务中尚不具备类人问题解决能力，揭示了高级推理与精细动作执行之间的鸿沟，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language(-Action) Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to interactive environments, yet existing benchmarks often overlook the complex physical reasoning required for point-and-click puzzle games. This paper introduces Vision-Language Against The Incredible Machine (VLATIM), a benchmark designed to evaluate human-like logical problem-solving capabilities within the classic physics puzzle game The Incredible Machine 2 (TIM). Unlike existing benchmarks, VLATIM specifically targets the critical gap between high-level logical reasoning and continuous action spaces requiring precise mouse interactions. This benchmark is structured into five progressive parts, assessing capabilities that range from basic visual grounding and domain understanding to multi-step manipulation and full puzzle solving. Our results reveal a significant disparity between reasoning and execution. While large proprietary models demonstrate superior planning abilities, they struggle with precise visual grounding. Consequently, they do not yet show human-like problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [244] [PIVOT: Bridging Planning and Execution in LLM Agents via Trajectory Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11225)
*Tuo Zhang,Alin-Ionut Popa,Yan Xu,Rui Song,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PIVOT是一个自监督框架，通过将轨迹作为可优化对象进行迭代细化，有效解决了LLM智能体规划与执行之间的不匹配问题，在DeepPlanning和GAIA上实现了SOTA性能，约束满足度相对提升最高达94%，且计算效率比同类方法高3-5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体经常生成看似合理但在执行时因不可行动作、约束违反和长程累积错误而失败的规划，存在严重的规划-执行不匹配问题。

Method: 提出PIVOT框架，包含四个阶段：PLAN（生成候选轨迹）、INSPECT（执行轨迹并计算结构化损失及文本梯度）、EVOLVE（根据反馈信号改进轨迹）、VERIFY（最终全局约束检查），并采用单调接受过程确保解质量非递减。

Result: 在DeepPlanning和GAIA上达到SOTA；HITL场景下约束满足度相对提升最高94%；全自主变体保持显著收益；计算效率比同类方法高3-5倍token消耗。

Conclusion: （自监督或人工监督的）基于反馈的轨迹优化是弥合自主智能体系统中规划-执行差距的一种原则性方法论。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents frequently generate seemingly coherent plans that fail upon execution due to infeasible actions, constraint violations, and compounding errors over extended horizons. PIVOT (Plan-Inspect-eVOlve Trajectories) addresses this plan-execution misalignment through a self-supervised framework that treats trajectories as optimizable objects iteratively refined via environment interaction. The framework comprises four stages: PLAN generates candidate trajectories; INSPECT executes them and computes structured losses with textual gradients encoding plan-execution discrepancies; EVOLVE applies these signals to produce improved trajectories; and VERIFY performs a final global check against task constraints. A monotonic acceptance process ensures a non-decreasing solution quality. Empirical evaluations on DeepPlanning and GAIA demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: with human-in-the-loop (HITL) feedback, PIVOT establishes a strong upper bound up to 94% relative improvement in constraint satisfaction, while its fully autonomous variant retains substantial gains, showing that the core trajectory-refinement mechanism remains effective without external supervision. At the same time, PIVOT remains computationally efficient, requiring up to 3x to 5x fewer tokens than competing refinement methods. These findings establish that (self- or human-supervised) feedback-based trajectory optimization is a principled methodology for mitigating plan-execution gaps in autonomous agent systems.

</details>


### [245] [The Semantic Training Gap: Ontology-Grounded Tool Architectures for Industrial AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11234)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM智造体在制造环境中缺乏操作语义理解的问题，本文提出了一种将制造本体直接嵌入AI工具层的架构，通过resolve、contextualize、annotate三操作接口契约和AIOps编排层强制语义约束，在实验中工具调用幻觉率从43%降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型驱动的AI智造体在制造环境中展现出领域术语的统计流利性，但缺乏对操作语义（即设备标识符、工艺参数、故障代码与法规约束之间的本体关系结构）的具身理解，导致即使语言上精确的输出在操作上却是错误的，且在多智造体配置中产生语义漂移的复合故障模式。

Method: 提出一种将制造本体直接嵌入AI工具层的架构：形式化为resolve（解析）、contextualize（语境化）、annotate（标注）三操作接口契约，通过AIOps编排层在运行时强制执行语义约束而非依赖模型训练。该架构以类型化关系配置的方式工作，使工具参数与领域本体对齐。

Result: 在覆盖六种行业配置的受控实验（72次工具调用，使用Qwen3-32B）中：无约束工具参数的领域标识符幻觉率为43%，基于本体的参数化方法将此降至0%。通过数字孪生分析平台验证，单一代码库配合领域特定本体配置即可消除工具调用幻觉，实现跨领域可配置性而无需更改应用代码。

Conclusion: 将本体嵌入工具层而非依赖模型训练，能够有效弥合LLM在制造领域的语义训练鸿沟，从根本上消除工具调用层面的幻觉，为多领域可配置的工业AI智造体提供了可行的架构方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based AI agents are increasingly deployed in manufacturing environments for analytics, quality management, and decision support. These agents demonstrate statistical fluency with domain terminology but lack grounded understanding of operational semantics -- the relational structure that connects equipment identifiers, process parameters, failure codes, and regulatory constraints within a specific production context. This paper identifies and formalizes the semantic training gap: a structural disconnect between how AI systems acquire domain vocabulary through training and how manufacturing operations define meaning through ontological relationships. We demonstrate that this gap causes operationally incorrect outputs even when model responses are linguistically precise, and that in multi-agent configurations it produces a compounding failure mode we term semantic drift. To close this gap, we present an architecture that embeds manufacturing ontology directly into the AI tool layer as a typed relational configuration, enforcing semantic constraints at runtime rather than relying on model training. The architecture is formalized as a three-operation interface contract -- resolve, contextualize, annotate -- with invariants enforced by an AIOps orchestration layer. In a controlled experiment across six industry configurations (72 tool invocations using Qwen3-32B), unconstrained tool parameters produced a 43% hallucination rate for domain identifiers; ontology-grounded parameters reduced this to 0%. We validate the approach through a digital twin analytics platform demonstrating that a single codebase with domain-specific ontology configurations eliminates tool-call hallucination and achieves cross-domain configurability without application code changes.

</details>


### [246] [Unlocking LLM Creativity in Science through Analogical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11258)
*Andrew Shen,Shaul Druckmann,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出类比推理（AR）方法，通过跨领域类比生成多样化解决方案，有效缓解LLM在开放性问题求解中的模式坍缩问题，并在四个生物医学任务上验证了其有效性，解决方案多样性提升90-173%，新颖方案生成率超50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主科学需要在开放性问题中持续生成新颖多样的解决方案，但现有LLM倾向于模式坍缩（mode collapse），生成结果多样性低。为解决这一瓶颈，亟需一种能引导模型跳出局部搜索、产生高多样性方案的方法。

Method: 提出类比推理（Analogical Reasoning, AR）方法：首先基于共享关系结构生成跨领域问题的类比，然后利用这些类比搜索新颖解决方案。AR不依赖领域内数据增强，而是通过结构对齐实现知识迁移。

Result: AR在多样性指标上提升90-173%，新颖方案生成率超过50%（基线最低仅1.6%）。在四个生物医学问题中：扰动效应预测分布指标提升近13倍；细胞通讯预测AUPRC超越所有基线；脑区交互推断与已发表方法Spearman ρ=0.729；寡核苷酸性质预测在两个数据集上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 类比推理是一种有效的开放性问题求解范式，可显著提升LLM生成解决方案的多样性与新颖性，并能增强现有方法搜索空间，具有实际应用可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous science promises to augment scientific discovery, particularly in complex fields like biomedicine. However, this requires AI systems that can consistently generate novel and diverse solutions to open-ended problems. We evaluate LLMs on the task of open-ended solution generation and quantify their tendency to mode collapse into low-diversity generations. To mitigate this mode collapse, we introduce analogical reasoning (AR) as a new approach to solution generation. AR generates analogies to cross-domain problems based on shared relational structure, then uses those analogies to search for novel solutions. Compared to baselines, AR discovers significantly more diverse generations (improving solution diversity metrics by 90-173%), generates novel solutions over 50% of the time (compared to as little as 1.6% for baselines), and produces high-quality analogies. To validate the real-world feasibility of AR, we implement AR-generated solutions across four biomedical problems, yielding consistent quantitative gains. AR-generated approaches achieve a nearly 13-fold improvement on distributional metrics for perturbation effect prediction, outperform all baselines on AUPRC when predicting cell-cell communication, infer brain region interactions with a high Spearman correlation ($ρ$=0.729) to published methods, and establish state-of-the-art performance on 2 datasets for oligonucleotide property prediction. The novel and diverse solutions produced by AR can be used to augment the search space of existing solution generation methods.

</details>


### [247] [LatentRouter: Can We Choose the Right Multimodal Model Before Seeing Its Answer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11301)
*Xueqi Cheng,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatentRouter提出了一种基于反事实多模态效用预测的MLLM路由方法，通过潜在路由胶囊、模型能力令牌和潜在通信来估计每个候选模型在给定查询上的表现，在MMR-Bench和VL-RouterBench上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型(MLLM)在OCR、图表理解、空间推理、视觉问答、成本和延迟等方面存在异构优势，因此有效的路由不仅需要估计查询难度，还需要将当前图像-问题输入的多模态需求与每个候选模型的能力相匹配。

Method: LatentRouter将MLLM路由公式化为反事实多模态效用预测。它提取学习到的多模态路由胶囊，用模型能力令牌表示每个候选MLLM，并在这些状态之间进行潜在通信来估计每个模型被选中时的表现。分布性结果头预测模型特定的反事实质量，有界胶囊校正机制在不允许残差信号主导预测的情况下细化边界决策。

Result: 在MMR-Bench和VL-RouterBench上的实验表明，LatentRouter优于固定模型、特征级和基于学习路由器的基线方法。增益在视觉、布局敏感或推理导向的多模态任务组上最为显著，潜在通信是改进的主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: LatentRouter通过潜在通信的反事实效用预测实现了有效的MLLM路由，支持性能导向和性能-成本路由，并能通过共享的每模型评分和可用性掩码处理变化的候选池。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have heterogeneous strengths across OCR, chart understanding, spatial reasoning, visual question answering, cost, and latency. Effective MLLM routing therefore requires more than estimating query difficulty: a router must match the multimodal requirements of the current image-question input with the capabilities of each candidate model. We propose LatentRouter, a router that formulates MLLM routing as counterfactual multimodal utility prediction. Given an image-question query, LatentRouter extracts learned multimodal routing capsules, represents each candidate MLLM with a model capability token, and performs latent communication between these states to estimate how each model would perform if selected. A distributional outcome head predicts model-specific counterfactual quality, while a bounded capsule correction refines close decisions without allowing residual signals to dominate the prediction. The resulting utility-based policy supports performance-oriented and performance-cost routing, and handles changing candidate pools through shared per-model scoring with availability masking. Experiments on MMR-Bench and VL-RouterBench show that LatentRouter outperforms fixed-model, feature-level, and learned-router baselines. Additional analyses show that the gains are strongest on multimodal task groups where model choice depends on visual, layout-sensitive, or reasoning-oriented requirements, and that latent communication is the main contributor to the improvement. The code is available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/LatentRouter.

</details>


### [248] [Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization: Utilizing Data Attribution for Effective Data Pruning in Low-Data Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11312)
*Danilo Brajovic,David A. Kreplin,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现Shapley值在低数据量剪枝场景中效果不佳，提出约束-数据-值-最大化(CDVM)方法，通过约束优化同时最大化总影响并惩罚过度的每测试贡献，在低数据保留率下实现稳健剪枝性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Shapley的数据价值方法在仅保留少量数据时，不适用于剪枝低价值数据的任务，需要更有效的方案。

Method: 提出CDVM方法，将剪枝建模为约束优化问题，同时最大化总影响和惩罚过度的每测试贡献，在低数据量场景下有效利用数据归因进行剪枝。

Result: 在OpenDataVal基准测试上，CDVM展现了强大的剪枝性能和具有竞争力的运行时间。

Conclusion: CDVM有效解决了Shapley值在低数据保留率下的剪枝局限性，为数据归因用于数据剪枝提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Attributing model behavior to training data is an evolving research field. A common benchmark is data removal, which involves eliminating data instances with either low or high values, then assessing a model's performance trained on the modified dataset. Many existing studies leverage Shapley-based data values for this task. In this paper, we demonstrate that these data values are not optimally suited for pruning low-value data when only a limited amount of data remains. To address this limitation, we introduce the Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization (CDVM) approach, which effectively utilizes data attributions for pruning in low-data scenarios. By casting pruning as a constrained optimization that both maximizes total influence and penalizes excessive per-test contributions, CDVM delivers robust performance when only a small fraction of the data is retained. On the OpenDataVal benchmark, CDVM shows strong performance and competitive runtime.

</details>


### [249] [Rethinking Evaluation for LLM Hallucination Detection: A Desiderata, A New RAG-based Benchmark, New Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11330)
*Wenbo Chen,Veena Padmanabhan,Tootiya Giyahchi,Elaine Wong,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了幻觉检测基准（HDB）应具备的理想属性，指出现有基准存在两大缺陷：缺乏基于RAG的长上下文基准，以及缺少真实标签噪声。为此构建了TRIVIA+基准，包含最长上下文样本及四种噪声标签方案。实验表明当前检测器仍有提升空间，LLM-as-a-Judge基线表现竞争力强，标签噪声会损害检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM幻觉检测基准存在两大缺陷：(1)缺乏基于RAG的长上下文基准（长文本阻碍人工标注）；(2)现有基准未提供真实的标签噪声用于压力测试，而现实场景常因人工或自动化标注存在噪声。因此需要构建一个满足所有理想属性的新基准。

Method: 首先建立幻觉检测基准（HDB）应具备的理想属性体系，基于此分析现有基准的不足。然后构建TRIVIA+基准，采用严格的人工标注流程，包含长上下文RAG样本，并设计四种噪声标签方案（样本相关与样本无关两种噪声模式）。最后在RAG-based HDB上使用SOTA检测器进行实验。

Result: (1)当前检测器在RAG-based HDB上远未达到性能上限；(2)基础的LLM-as-a-Judge基线表现具有竞争力；(3)标签噪声会显著损害检测性能。TRIVIA+包含文献中最长上下文的样本。

Conclusion: 现有幻觉检测基准未能满足所有理想属性，TRIVIA+填补了长上下文RAG基准和标签噪声测试的空白。实验结果揭示了当前检测器的不足，呼吁加强对RAG任务中幻觉检测的研究。

Abstract: Hallucination, broadly referring to unfaithful, fabricated, or inconsistent content generated by LLMs, has wide-ranging implications. Therefore, a large body of effort has been devoted to detecting LLM hallucinations, as well as designing benchmark datasets for evaluating these detectors. In this work, we first establish a desiderata of properties for hallucination detection benchmarks (HDBs) to exhibit for effective evaluation. A critical look at existing HDBs through the lens of our desiderata reveals that none of them exhibits all the properties. We identify two largest gaps: (1) RAG-based grounded benchmarks with long context are severely lacking (partly because length impedes human annotation); and (2) Existing benchmarks do not make available realistic label noise for stress-testing detectors although real-world use-cases often grapple with label noise due to human or automated/weak annotation. To close these gaps, we build and open-source a new RAG-based HDB called T RIVIA+ that underwent a rigorous human annotation process. Notably, our benchmark exhibits all desirable properties including (1) T RIVIA+ contains samples with the longest context in the literature; and (2) we design and share four sets of noisy labels with different, both sample-dependent and sampleindependent, noise schemes. Finally, we perform experiments on RAG-based HDBs, including our T RIVIA+, using popular SOTA detectors that reveal new insights: (i) ample room remains for current detectors to reach the performance ceiling on RAG-based HDBs, (ii) the basic LLM-as-a-Judge baseline performs competitively, and (iii) label noise hinders detection performance. We expect that our findings, along with our proposed benchmark 1 , will motivate and foster needed research on hallucination detection for RAG-based tasks.

</details>


### [250] [CPEMH: An Agentic Framework for Prompt-Driven Behavior Evaluation and Assurance in Foundation-Model Systems for Mental Health Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11341)
*Giuliano Lorenzoni,Ivens Portugal,Paulo Alencar,Donald Cowan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CPEMH是一个用于评估基础模型在心理健康筛查中提示驱动行为的工程框架，通过模块化编排架构实现提示策略的自主设计、评估与选择，确保行为可追溯性、可复现性与鲁棒性。在抑郁症自动筛查案例中验证了其稳定化与审计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模语言系统在心理健康筛查等敏感场景中行为变异性不可控的问题，缺乏系统化的行为保证方法论来评估和稳定基础模型的提示驱动行为。

Method: 提出CPEMH框架，包含编排器、推理和评估三种模块化智能体，自主执行提示策略的设计、评估与选择全流程，形成系统化的行为控制工程方法。

Result: 在抑郁症自动筛查案例中验证了框架稳定和审计基础模型行为的能力，实现了行为变异的系统化控制。

Conclusion: 模块化编排在行为保证中起关键作用；应优先保障稳定性而非增加架构复杂度；需将F1、偏差和鲁棒性作为核心验收标准。

Abstract: This paper presents CPEMH, an agentic framework designed to evaluate prompt-driven behavior in foundation-model systems operating on transcript-based datasets for mental-health screening. CPEMH serves as an engineering methodology for behavioral assurance in large-scale language systems, introducing an orchestrated architecture that autonomously performs the design, evaluation, and selection of prompt strategies, enabling systematic control of behavioral variability across contexts. Its modular agentic design, combining orchestrator, inference, and evaluation agents, ensures traceability, reproducibility, and robustness throughout the prompting lifecycle. A case study on automated depression screening from interview transcripts demonstrates the framework's capacity to stabilize and audit foundation-model behavior in conversational and clinically sensitive domains. Lessons learned emphasize the role of modular orchestration in behavioral assurance, the prioritization of stability over architectural complexity, and the integration of F1, bias, and robustness as core acceptance criteria.

</details>


### [251] [CVEvolve: Autonomous Algorithm Discovery for Unstructured Scientific Data Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11359)
*Ming Du,Xiangyu Yin,Yanqi Luo,Dishant Beniwal,Songyuan Tang,Hemant Sharma,Mathew J. Cherukara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CVEvolve是一个零代码自主智能体框架，通过多轮搜索策略和代码执行、评估、历史管理等工具，帮助领域科学家自动发现科学数据处理算法。在X射线荧光显微镜图像配准、布拉格峰值检测和高能衍射显微镜图像分割三个任务上，CVEvolve发现了优于基线方法的算法，表明零代码、LLM驱动的自主算法开发能有效辅助科学家从非结构化图像数据中获取实用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据处理通常需要特定任务的算法或AI模型，这给缺乏计算或图像处理专业知识的领域科学家造成了障碍，尤其是在数据存在噪声、高动态范围、稀疏标注或仅松散指定的情况下。

Method: CVEvolve是一个自主智能体框架，提供零代码接口，结合多轮搜索策略，包含代码执行、评估实现、历史管理、留出测试以及科学数据和视觉输出的可选检查等工具。搜索过程在发现和改进动作之间交替，使用谱系感知的随机候选采样来平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在X射线荧光显微镜图像配准、布拉格峰值检测和高能衍射显微镜图像分割三个任务上，CVEvolve发现的算法优于基线方法，留出测试跟踪有助于识别泛化能力更强的候选算法，避免过优化。

Conclusion: 零代码、自主LLM驱动的算法开发能够帮助领域科学家将非结构化科学图像数据转化为实用算法，促进下游科学发现。

Abstract: Scientific data processing often requires task-specific algorithms or AI models, creating a barrier for domain scientists who need to analyze their data but may not have extensive computing or image-processing expertise. This barrier is especially pronounced when data are noisy, have a high dynamic range, are sparsely labeled, or are only loosely specified. We introduce CVEvolve, an autonomous agentic harness with a zero-code interface for scientific data-processing algorithm discovery. CVEvolve combines a multi-round search strategy with tools for code execution, evaluation implementation, history management, holdout testing, and optional inspection of scientific data and visual outputs. The search alternates between discovery and improvement actions, and uses lineage-aware stochastic candidate sampling to balance exploration and exploitation. We demonstrate CVEvolve on x-ray fluorescence microscopy image registration, Bragg peak detection, and high-energy diffraction microscopy image segmentation. Across these tasks, CVEvolve discovers algorithms that improve over baseline methods, while holdout test tracking helps identify candidates that generalize better than later over-optimized alternatives. These results show that zero-code, autonomous LLM-powered algorithm development can help domain scientists turn unstructured scientific image data into practical algorithms and downstream scientific discoveries.

</details>


### [252] [Causal Bias Detection in Generative Artifical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11365)
*Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了生成式AI中的因果公平性问题，将其与标准机器学习设置统一在共同理论框架下，推导了新的因果分解方法以实现细粒度公平性量化，并提出了识别条件与高效估计量，通过大语言模型中的种族与性别偏差分析验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果公平性研究主要针对标准机器学习场景（学习单个预测函数），而生成式AI可对任意变量集进行条件采样，隐式构建所有因果机制，这种根本性差异要求发展新的因果公平性方法。
